# Zforge — regression coverage matrix

## 21-language expansion — 2026-10-11 (validation in progress)

- Baseline: `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationBaseline.xcresult`, log `/private/tmp/zforge-localization-baseline.log`: **318 cases / 416 executions**, **316 cases passed, 2 failed**, zero skipped. Failures: `ArchiveIndexTests.grantChangeNotification` (0 vs 1) and `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` (0.143292416 s vs below 100 ms), both before language changes. Existing Siri test weak-variable diagnostics, test-target metadata extraction notice and FileOperationServiceTests QoS runtime warning are separate baseline diagnostics.
- Failing-before localization characterization: `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationBefore.xcresult`: **3 cases / 10 executions, all failed** for exactly the missing 21-language choices, missing localized Open strings and the English symbolic password error.
- Prepared infrastructure: signed Debug build passes (`/private/tmp/zforge-localization-build.log`). `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationPrepared.xcresult` executes **9 cases / 65 executions: 5 cases / 28 executions pass; 4 cases / 37 executions fail**, zero skipped. Passing contracts: exactly 21 languages plus system, preference ordering/English fallback, all 22 saved preference round-trips, legacy/corrupt preferences and readable English password error. Failures remain for absent new translations, Chinese localized content, interpolated service errors and complete bundled catalog coverage. Chinese resolution assertions themselves pass. A narrower method filter produced **zero tests** in `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationResolution.xcresult`; its green command is not acceptance.
- Complete scalar catalog characterization: `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationScalar.xcresult`: **10 cases / 77 executions, 8 cases / 70 executions passed, 2 cases / 7 executions failed**, zero skipped. Six failures reproduced missing Slavic plural grammar. The seventh exposed an incorrect new Dutch test fixture: it assumed `Openen`, whereas the native Finder action is `Open` (`nl.lproj/LocalizableMerged.strings`, `N151`). The native-UX requirement and LOCALIZATION.md justify correcting that expected label in both new tests; complete resource/parameter coverage is retained independently, so an English-looking homonym cannot hide a missing Dutch resource.
- Grammatical variations and multiargument confirmations: `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationFinal.xcresult` passes **11 cases / 87 executions**, zero failed/skipped. All 415 keys × 21 localizations (8,715 entries), all plural branches and ordered substitutions are independently audited before catalog write and from compiled bundle resources.
- Exact final shared-source confirmation: `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationVerified.xcresult`, log `/private/tmp/zforge-localization-verified.log`, passes **11 cases / 87 executions**, zero failures/skips. The signed Debug application and test targets compile successfully; the focused suite completes in 0.143 seconds. The catalog has **8,845 string units**, including **180 plural branches**. `git diff --check` passes. This final focused confirmation does not replace the full regression and native UI boundaries below.
- Full regression: `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationRegression.xcresult`: **335 cases / 509 executions, 327 cases / 499 executions passed, 8 cases / 10 executions failed**, zero skipped. Failures concern TagCatalogTests discovery/checkpoint/replay/link/staged-authorization. Initial suspicion that the catalog audit caused the eight-second delay was not established: its focused duration was **0.146 seconds**, while its full-suite duration included shared-executor delay. The resource audit now explicitly runs concurrently off the main thread (an executed assertion verifies this) and aggregates all value/argument violations without dropping any conditions or variants. `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationRegressionFastAudit.xcresult` still passes every localization case but reports **335 cases / 509 executions, 328 cases / 500 executions passed, 7 cases / 9 executions failed** in tag persistence. These failures remain unresolved and are not presented as localization acceptance or conclusively assigned to its code.
- Intermediate isolation experiments: `RegressionIsolated` and `RegressionConcurrent` retain tag failures; the first aggregate-report attempt (`RegressionAudit`) fails compilation because Foundation prohibits querying Thread.isMainThread directly from async code, **zero tests executed**. A synchronous nonisolated query helper fixes that diagnostic. All original assertions/scenarios/time bounds remain represented. The shared worktree added six ArchiveIndexTests cases since the 318-case baseline; they are distinct from the eleven localization cases.
- The isolated TagCatalogTests diagnostic and a first alternate-bundle execution did not establish an IDE test session; they were cancelled without test acceptance. The first alternate build gave all targets the same bundle identifier; the interface validation now uses distinct identifiers derived from TARGET_NAME. An automatically opened validation instance requested Home access; no personal-folder grant was made. The installed Zforge process is preserved.
- Commit-scope verification: `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationCommitValidation.xcresult`, log `/private/tmp/zforge-localization-commit-validation.log`, passes **11 cases / 87 executions**, zero failures/skips, in a temporary copy exported from the prepared Git index. Parallel automation/index changes, build-version bumps and the later untranslated `Try Again` key are excluded. The staged catalog preserves existing Xcode formatting and has exactly the same parsed values as the tested snapshot. This establishes focused coverage for the independent localization commit without claiming UI or complete-regression approval.
- Controlled localization exclusion: `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationTagControl.xcresult` executes **57 TagCatalogTests cases / 71 executions: 50 cases / 62 executions pass, 7 cases / 9 executions fail**, zero skipped. The temporary project preserves the current shared application/test source, restores only FileBrowserPreferences, AppLocalization and ZforgeApp to their pre-localization versions, restores the original two-language catalog, and excludes only the two new localization test files. The same seven tag cases and checkpoint failures occur without localization changes. This establishes that the observed tag failures do not require this localization implementation; their underlying cause remains unresolved. No production tag code or existing expectations were changed.
- Native UI infrastructure: `/private/tmp/ZforgeLocalizationUIFreshService.xcresult` fails before executing the Settings/menu scenario with **Timed out while enabling automation mode**. The result summary's one failure represents runner initialization, **zero application scenarios executed**. A fresh user testmanagerd reproduces the blocker; its system log identifies LocalAuthentication awaiting **Enable UI Automation** authentication. No authentication or security-setting bypass was attempted. The installed Zforge instance remains untouched.
- `LocalizationUITests.testLanguageSelectionUpdatesOpenMenusInAllBrowserModesAndPersists` is added for native Settings selection across all 21 languages, all four modes and reopening persistence; **execution blocked by native automation authentication**. Own authoring and Google/DeepL comparison are complete with the limits in LOCALIZATION.md. Full themes/accessibility and independent human linguistic review remain separate.

This matrix links `Documentation/Requirements/CURRENT-STATE.md` contracts to automated testing and verifications that depend on macOS. It describes **covering of scenarios**, not percentage of lines. Existing test means identified source; execution and result must be recorded separately. In a refactoring, add the characterization case before changing the corresponding area.

## Compulsory guarantee policy

Since **2026-10-04**, apply the [requirements RNF-Q01 to RNF-Q06](Documentation/Requirements/PRODUCT-REQUIREMENTS.md#quality-polishing-and-regression-prevention) and [test rules for agents](AGENTS.md#tests-as-a-behavioral-guarantee). The policy is normative; this documentary update does not add tests performed or solve the gaps of this matrix.

- For each changed functionality, relate requirement, use scenario (initial status → action → result), relevant unit tests/integration/UI and necessary visual or performance checks. It shall also cover shared state/handler consumers and adjacent affected flows.
- Corrections shall include a test that reproduces failure before the change and then passes; refactorings must preserve the previous characterization. Changes in the logic/expectations of existing tests require demonstrated disagreement with the specification and traceable justification. Infrastructure adjustments cannot reduce scenarios or rigor.
- Record what you actually executed, results, failures, ignored, environment and limits. Built approved, written only test or runner who did not initiate do not approve a contract. Visual checks and performance measurements have their own evidence.
- Regression introduced or reintroduced into consolidated functionality blocks the conclusion. Do not hide failures pre-existing or declare full warranty when the necessary verification remains pending.

## Contract matrix

ZF-39 workload defined before measurement: group and undo 1,000 sibling text files in a local temporary directory, preserving every byte, for three iterations. Compare grouping latency with the existing `FileOperationService.transfer(..., .move)` on the same files. Local acceptance target: median grouping below one second and no more than twice the baseline median. This fixture measures filesystem work only; UI frame pacing, CPU/memory, external volumes, and large-selection accessibility remain separate checks.

Execution evidence below; multiple external application launches and the full themes/accessibility matrix remain separate checks. |

## Execution of ZF-08 in 2026-09-23

- The suite `ZforgeTests` passed with **80 cases, 0 failures** in the build Debug without signature, after splitting the modes into own files. Result: `/private/tmp/zforge-zf08-postsplit-unit/Logs/Test/Test-Zforge-2026.09.23_20-30-14--0300.xcresult`.
- The signed suite `ZforgeUITests` passed with**11 distinct cases, 0 failures** (14 executions counting the repetitions of the launch test). Result: `/private/tmp/zforge-zf08-ui-signed/Logs/Test/Test-Zforge-2026.09.23_20-30-41--0300.xcresult`. Without signature, the runner closed with `Early unexpected exit ... signal kill before establishing connection`; use the project signature setting to test the UI in this host.
- In the built Debug opened by Launch Services, a ZIP fixture created dedicated window and displayed its contents. In another window with fixtures, `⌘=` changed the accessible value of List from 100% to 110%; the toolbar and the secondary click presented Open, Extract Here, Extract to the named folder, Extract... and Validate to a archive. The drag visually moved the name and preview column divider. The switch to Icons, Columns and Gallery displayed the respective surfaces with 110% zoom.
- The Run button of the Xcode started the Zforge scheme and displayed `Running Zforge`. There is still a reliable inspection that links the window shown to the exact instance initiated by Xcode, as there are other instances of the same bundle in the environment. The coordinate drag XCTest did not reach the handle; the change in width was confirmed in the interface controlled directly and in unit calculations.

## Static integration of 7-Zip — ZF-21 in 2026-10-03

- Platform: app and engine exclusively arm64, deployment target macOS 26.0. The [plan](Documentation/Requirements/SEVENZIP-STATIC-INTEGRATION-PLAN.md) records the implementation and checks still open.
- Previous reference: 98 approved tests, 0 failures and 0 ignored (`/private/tmp/ZforgeStaticBaselineAllowed.xcresult`). Native final suite: **108 approved tests, 0 failures and 0 ignored**, confirmed by the summary of `/private/tmp/ZforgeNativeAcceptanceFixed.xcresult`; log `/private/tmp/zforge-native-acceptance-fixed.log`. Xcode 27.0, SDK macOS 27.0, host macOS 27.0.1 arm64; does not equal the execution in macOS 26.0.
- `NativeSevenZipTests` adds real operations with Unicode/emoji names, delimiters and line breaks; limit of bytes effectively extracted; prior cancellation; rejection of traversal and external symbolic destiny; invalid CRC; symbolic links ZIP/7z/TAR; divided volumes ZIP/7z; real advanced options and presence of 60 handlers, including RAR/RAR5. Existing tests now use the native engine, preserving navigation/ringing, creating the seven formats, extraction, transactional update ZIP/7z and protection of the original. Encrypted update preserves AES and encrypted names. Handler log does not confirm reading all formats; previous cancellation does not prove all moments of interruption.
- Debug and Release compiled with the script sandbox enabled. Release final em `/private/tmp/ZforgeNativeRelease/Build/Products/Release/Zforge.app`; log `/private/tmp/zforge-native-release-final.log`. Mach-O Inspection: arm64, `minos 26.0`, system dependencies without 7-Zip dylib. Bundle contains the main executable and licenses without `7zz`. Probe relinked against `.a` Release with dead stripping returned 60 formats; `nm` confirmed the factory/registration functions in the final executable. Checking `codesign --verify --deep --strict` of the local Apple Development subscription and effective App Sandbox entries, chosen files and application scope bookmarks; does not prove Developer ID, notation or acceptance in the store.
- Recompilation/relinking test in `/private/tmp/ZforgeStaticRelinkProof`: independent copy of the vendored sources, marking function added to the engine, `.a` recompiled and relinked probe performing the result `2602`. Build and relink instructions for the app in [BuildSupport/SevenZip/README.md](BuildSupport/SevenZip/README.md). This proof is neither public publication of LGPL materials nor execution of the entire app with a modified version.
- Real UI attempt in `/private/tmp/ZforgeNativeUIActual.xcresult`: two cases failed to activate the app (`Running Background`) before confirming the flows. The previous round that selected incorrect names ran zero cases and does not count as coverage. ### Engine Microbenchmark

Three sequential executions, heated cache, ZIP Store, core native O2 versus `Vendor/7zz` official 26.02; medians in seconds. It does not include staging Swift, UI, sandbox or cold cache. Local artifacts: `/private/tmp/ZforgeNativeBenchmark/results.json`, `/private/tmp/zforge-native-benchmark.mm` and `/private/tmp/zforge-run-benchmark.py`.

| Dataset / motor | Criar | Listar | Testar | Extrair |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| A file of 64 MiB / native | 0,0241 | 0,0006 | 0,0089 | 0,0225 |
| A file of 64 MiB / CLI | 0,0254 | 0,0034 | 0,0114 | 0,0228 |
| 2.000 files of 256 B / native | 0,3015 | 0,0018 | 0,0042 | 0,1670 |
| 2.000 files of 256 B / CLI | 0,3109 | 0,1478 | 0,0076 | 0,1290 |

The native extraction of small files remained approximately 30% slower in this sample, even after reducing directory openings with safe descriptor cache. Listing prevents process startup and textual parsing. Do not complete overall application performance from this sample. Maximum HT native was between 13–17 MB; the CLI processes measured by operation were between 4–8 MB, with different life cycles, so do not establish comparison of memory of the full app. Point-to-point benchmark remains in OR-10.

## Criteria for use

1. Associate each change to a matrix line and point out the existing test, the new test or manual script executed.
2. Rotate the suite of the area, then the full suites, and register in the summary of the change the results with the execution path.
3. If an automation depends on Spotlight, PowerBox, Finder, Quick Look or drag between apps and is unstable in the runner, maintain deterministic test for the internal rule and register real manual validation. Do not mark as automated which has only been inspected or compiled.

## 2026-10-03 — deterministic synchronization of the rapid selection test in Columns

New cancellation after fulfilling all promises preserves origins; Custom policy uses empty writingOptions.

## Refined drag plane of archives — ZF-30 / D-33 in 2026-10-04

New evidence **user-related**: deletion and badge copy/Option work; Operations are still dead. Preview differs from the local item and part of the top of the screen. This interaction only updates requirements/plan; No new tests or build. Previous results do not cover the expanded contract.

Coverage required in the next implementation, currently pending:

| Contract | Verification required |
| --- | --- |
| Appearance in full equal to the site | Side-by-side comparison in List/Icons/Columns/Gallery, zoom, multiple selection, catch point and start/cancell/conclusion animations; no large icon replacing line and jump from the top |
| Copy Out | real Gesture, Bytes/Content at the exact destination, name without ancestors and origin intact; folder/empty area/sidebar/other window/Finder/Desktop |
| Move Out with Option | Confirmation of the original archive, real extraction and removal only after success; source stored in failure/cancellation |
| Keyboard in Confirmation | Return/Enter approves, Escape cancels, Tab/Shift+Tab/Space operate controls; no destination/original changed when canceling before execution; dialogue closing preserves request approved |
| Move within your own archive | No option, correct destination root/folder, source removed and original validated after a complete update |
| Copy inside your own archive | with Option, content in the internal destination and conserved origin; confirmation of change and conflicts without silent loss |
| Context and modifiers | Toggle internal/external destination and option during gesture; badge/proposal correspond to the decision captured when dropping, including when the external receiver uses file promises |
| Integrity and freshness | Folders/descendants, selected father/child, conflicts, password, failures, cancellation, obsolete session, authorizations, cycles and movement to the same folder; preserve expansion/navigation of others archives and reveal the result |
| Adjacent flows | Drag local, local addition to archive, selection/clicks/double click, menus/toolbar/keyboard, disclosure, preview, languages and resize |

Source: [plan current](Documentation/Requirements/ARCHIVE-DRAG-DROP-PLAN.md). Do not replace the result check with the acceptance of drop, badge, build or direct motor/receiver tests.

| Contract | Evidence Executed |
| --- | --- |
| Output ZIP/7z copy default / Option move | `outboundTransfer`, `nativeDestination`, `outboundPromises`, `dragOperation`; exact content in destination, absence of ancestors, source/tree preserved in copying and removal after complete publication |
| Sequential Promises and Cancellation | `outboundPromises`, `cancelledOutboundPromises`, `rejectedPromise`; each callback concludes before the next request/ordered, confirmation before recording, no written if refused, intact origins if cancelled after publications |
| Partial failure and publication | `partialExportFailure`, `exportFolderAndFailure`; all the originals kept. Internal receivers extract into a hidden sibling on the destination volume and rename it; an external promise is written by the bridge exactly at the authorized URL, without access to the parent folder (ZF-55 stage 1: `ArchiveInPlaceReadingTests.exportPublishesDirectly`) |
| Internal reorganisation ZIP/7z protected | `internalTransfers` parameterized copy/move; payload/survivors and password preserved, headers 7z continue to require password |
| Conflicts, cycles, multiple selection and names | `internalConflict`, `sameParent`, `foldersAndCycles`, `equalNamesInSelection`; maintain/cancel does not modify bytes, replace validated, normalized parent/child, folders/descendants, distinct name for copy in the same parent and for homonymous sources, cycles refused |
| Safety/cancellation/concurrent change | `internalSafety`, `concurrentReorganization`, `cancelledReorganization`, `refusedRemoval`; old session, wrong password, editing during resolution and cancellation do not replace the original |
| Confirmation before writing | `rejectedConfirmation`, `rejectedPromise`; request conserves archive/quantity/destination/operation, Return/scape on buttons, cancellation without destination or change of original |
| Update and selection in all four modes | `internalRefresh` (8 executions), output tests and pre-existing tests `archiveAdditionPreservesInlineHierarchy`/`archiveAdditionAfterDialogDismissal`; new session, surviving directories, revealed/selected destination, only invalidated archive |
| Destination policy and geometry | `internalPolicyAndFormation`; without option move/with option copy internally, inversion out, mode formation and folder destination more specific than overlay of the free area |
| Real Gesture in Four Modes | `testArchiveDragCopiesIntoDroppedFolderInEveryMode`; real files ZIP/Inside/Payload.txt → Dropped directory, opened after gesture, visible result at List/Icons/Columns/Gallery |
| Move Out / Keyboard | `testArchiveMoveUsesKeyboardConfirmationAndCancellation`; Option, Escape without file in destination, new Return attempt and extraction in destination |
| Internal reorganization / expansion | `testArchiveInternalMoveAndOptionCopyRevealExpandedDestination`; move to Target, Return, result revealed; Option to copy to Inside, Return, two visible entries |
| Another sandbox process | `testArchivePromisesPublishIntoFinderBeforeRemovingOriginal`; Finder receives copy/move, real file contains `payload`, source preserved in copy and absent after confirmed movement. Directory belongs to the runner outside the app container |
| Outra janela | `testArchiveCopyIntoAnotherZforgeWindow`; gesture between two windows, destination folder opened with new file, source still present |
| Preserved local flows | `testLocalDragRetainsMoveAndOptionCopyBehavior` and `testLocalDropIntoArchiveRetainsConfirmationAndSource`; without option moves, option copies, drop at ZIP confirms addition and conserves source; `testArchiveRemovalIsSharedByToolbarAndContextMenu` Covers action in both menus |

Complete unit suite, not just engine filters. The `xcresulttool` records 172 cases / 197 executions in the set with UI.

## Documentation language migration — 2026-10-05

The 138 documents in the Portuguese-content inventory were translated into English at the user's request. This is a documentation migration; existing implementation status, historical evidence, and pending functional checks retain their meaning. The documentation-language contract is recorded in `Documentation/Requirements/DOCUMENTATION-REQUIREMENTS.md`.

Validation performed for this migration:

- All 138 inventoried documents changed; structural comparisons passed for table nesting/cell counts, requirement/task IDs, numerical literals, measurement units, hashes, and existing inline code. Search/template placeholders were translated intentionally; the added language contract is accounted for separately.
- All 11 fenced blocks were preserved, except the translated static-core label in the Mermaid diagram. Actual commands and executable examples were preserved. All 109 documents with primary-source front matter retained that metadata unchanged; the context template's placeholder descriptions were translated separately.
- The 4492 Markdown link destinations retained their paths and actual external URLs; links to renamed local headings were updated. This comparison preserves preexisting references and does not claim that every historical external page or missing asset was revalidated.
- Nine temporary, deterministic fixture tests for `Documentation/SwiftUI/tools/bootstrap.py` passed: English sections and field labels, primary-source text and provenance, missing-module and empty-content fallbacks, empty link lists, local/external links, and English index generation. Zero failures or skipped cases. These are generator checks, not application tests, and are not a new committed regression suite.

No application build, unit suite, UI run, or performance measurement was performed for this documentation-only change. Existing functional-validation gaps remain open. No corpus recapture or crawl was performed; translating local prose does not establish new Apple-source evidence.

## 2026-10-05 — New Folder with Selection (ZF-39)

Contract: [CURRENT-STATE](Documentation/Requirements/CURRENT-STATE.md), [UI-UX-GUIDE](Documentation/Requirements/UI-UX-GUIDE.md). Holding Option replaces New Folder in File; `⌃⌥⌘N` groups eligible local siblings and focuses inline rename. `⇧⌘N` remains the empty-folder command. The native alternate and direct shortcut use the same state handler with independent shortcut registration: assigning Control to the alternate's shortcut prevented replacement on Option alone. The alternate retains AppKit's inherited `⌥⇧⌘N` equivalent; the direct shortcut is also documented in Help.

`FolderWithSelectionTests` adds 13 cases (16 parameterized executions): files/folders and untouched items, unique names, complete rollback on failed moves, preserved recovery content when restoration fails, cancellation after the first move, invalid/missing/duplicate/different-parent selections, exact Undo/Redo with conflicts and added content, rename around grouping, all four state modes, opened Columns branches, and navigation during suspended refresh. Its last scenario failed before the stale-context guard was added and passed afterward. These tests assert filesystem content and observable state, not only return values.

| Execution | Actual result and scope |
| --- | --- |
| `/tmp/ZforgeFolderSelection-baseline.xcresult` | 16 existing file-operation cases passed before implementation. |
| `/tmp/ZforgeFolderSelection-menu-before.xcresult` | New UI contract failed because New Folder with Selection was absent before implementation. |
| `/tmp/ZforgeFolderSelection-navigation-before.xcresult` | Controlled navigation-during-refresh test failed with stale rename state before its guard was corrected. The round also encountered the original hosting-window teardown crash. |
| `/tmp/ZforgeFolderSelection-regression.xcresult` | Full unit suite: **181 passed, 1 failed, 0 skipped** out of 182 distinct cases; **247 passed, 1 failed** out of 248 parameterized executions. All 13 new cases/16 executions and all 16 existing file-operation cases passed. The failure is D-28: `.timedOut("started:First.zip")` in rapid Columns archive selection. This is a failed full suite, not general regression approval. |
| `/tmp/ZforgeFolderSelection-column-diagnostic.xcresult` | Incorrect initial filter executed **0 tests**; excluded from functional evidence despite the successful command exit. |
| `/tmp/ZforgeFolderSelection-column-diagnostic2.xcresult` | Correct complete identifier executed the unchanged Columns case once: **1 passed, 0 failed/skipped**. This diagnostic does not approve the failed full run or establish the timeout's cause. |
| `/tmp/ZforgeFolderSelection-ui9.xcresult` | Complete grouping UI case passed: file plus archive selected through clicks, activation by direct shortcut and by the enabled Option menu, focused rename, opening and checking both grouped items, Back and absence of originals, then original empty-folder shortcut and Escape. Both activation paths passed in Icons/List/Columns/Gallery at 1100 pt (eight flows). Two other cases failed on fixture/input issues subsequently corrected. |
| `/tmp/ZforgeFolderSelection-ui10.xcresult` | Option press/release and disabled empty-selection UI case passed in all four modes; existing independent-window mode case passed. Portuguese Help input lost the `c` in simulated typing; existing Help lacked an initial browser window. These two failures were not accepted as approval. |
| `/tmp/ZforgeFolderSelection-ui11.xcresult` | Portuguese menu/help case passed. Existing English Help failed when XCTest bound an interruption to windows from another app instance, `io.libor.Zforge.IconPreviewValidation`. |
| `/tmp/ZforgeFolderSelection-ui12.xcresult` | Portuguese menu/help and existing English Help filtering: **2 passed, 0 failed/skipped**. Final relevant UI evidence comprises **five distinct passed cases** across ui9/ui10/ui12, not the sum of retries. |

Debug builds completed on macOS 27.0.1/arm64. UI validation used a build-only `PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER=io.libor.Zforge.FolderSelectionCheck` override to isolate fixtures from concurrent app instances; project identifiers remain unchanged. The original-identifier build ran the full unit suite. Catalog JSON validation and `git diff --check` passed.

Fixture/accessibility corrections preserved the assertions and scenarios: wait for native selected traits while holding Command, because single-click recognition follows the double-click decision; expose the Icons rename text field instead of combining it into an inaccessible parent; wait for an actual browser window rather than an arbitrary restored Help window; close only the fixture browser before searching Help; supply the entire Unicode query by native paste and assert its exact value. The clipboard is restored only if it has not changed concurrently. Original hosting windows are detached on MainActor before closing to avoid deferred AppKit lifecycle callbacks; focus assertions remain unchanged. Gallery empty selection is cleared in the thumbnail strip's blank area, rather than in the populated preview.

Visual review: exported ui10 screenshots confirm the same File position, correct disabled state, replacement with Option, and restoration after release in all four modes. Exported ui12 screenshots confirm the Portuguese alternate at 680 pt and the complete localized Help row with `⌃⌥⌘N`, without clipping. The original explicit-Control alternate failed the visible replacement check even though its hidden accessibility item existed; its independent shortcut registration fixes that distinction. These static captures do not establish animation fluidity or the broader accessibility matrix.

Performance: `largeSelectionWorkload` ran the predeclared 1,000-file workload for three iterations, grouping and restoring every file and checking its original bytes. Existing move baseline: 570.6/648.3/640.6 ms; grouping: 839.9/1008.6/770.7 ms. Median **839.9 ms**, versus **640.6 ms** baseline (**1.31×**), meets the local median target below one second and at most twice baseline. This was a local temporary-directory measurement during the unit suite, not a CPU/memory, external-volume, or UI frame-pacing benchmark.

Remaining checks are OR-17: external volume/authorization failure paths, actual Undo/Redo and recovery UI, light theme, zoom, VoiceOver/reduced motion, and UI responsiveness/CPU/memory. D-28 investigation remains open; its historical intermittence is documented separately, and no expectations, timeout, or production Columns handler were changed to hide this failure.


## Last-closed-window view restoration and build diagnostics — 2026-10-05 (ZF-37)

- Contract: window modes remain independent; the last browser window/tab closed supplies the next window/application startup mode. Normal Quit with windows open uses the frontmost remaining browser. Settings/Info/help and scene replacement do not overwrite the saved mode.

- Before enabling the native close save, `/tmp/zforge-view-unit-reproduction.xcresult` executed **6 cases / 16 executions**: **7 passed, 9 failed**. All eight close-order/mode combinations and scene-replacement closure failed because the preference retained the previous mode; independence, explicit Settings defaults, preview lookup, and four Quit modes passed. The temporary missing-save reproduction was removed before final validation.
- UI reproduction `/tmp/zforge-view-reproduction.xcresult`: **zero UI cases executed**. The runner failed to initialize with `Timed out while enabling automation mode`; this is not functional approval.
- The first complete unit launch (`/tmp/zforge-view-final-unit.log`) compiled with Swift/C/C++ warnings treated as errors, but the test daemon invalidated its XPC control connection and timed out before starting tests. That launch was stopped. A SIGTERM recovery attempt left the daemon running; `launchctl kickstart` was denied by SIP, which remained enabled. Terminating the unresponsive per-user daemon allowed launchd to start a new instance. No assertions, scenarios, retries, or timeouts were weakened. Final execution is recorded below.

The resulting window used List both in-process and after relaunch. This is a bounded actual-use check, not a passed XCTest UI run. No files were modified by these interactions.
- Final `/tmp/zforge-view-regression.xcresult`: **172 distinct unit cases / 245 executions passed**, zero failures or skipped cases. This includes all **6 WindowViewModeTests cases / 16 executions**, all four modes in both close orders, four Quit modes, replacement/unrelated-window checks, and the complete archive mutation, preview, navigation, menu-state, preferences, and file-operation suite. Swift and C/C++ compiler warnings were treated as errors.
- The full strict Debug compilation (`/tmp/zforge-view-final-unit.log`) emitted **zero source compiler warnings/errors**. The existing AppIntents metadata-tool notice (`Metadata extraction skipped, no AppIntents.framework dependency found`) was present before and after the change; no new diagnostic was accepted.
- Final UI `/tmp/zforge-view-final-ui.xcresult`: **3 distinct cases / 3 executions passed**, zero failures or skipped cases. `testLastClosedWindowRestoresViewModeAfterRelaunch` restores Icons, List, Columns, and Gallery after two-window closure, with both close orders, a new in-process window, normal Quit, and relaunch without a view override. The new relaunch test uses a unique UserDefaults suite to isolate persisted fixture settings from user preferences. `testViewModeIsIndependentBetweenWindows` preserves command/toolbar isolation; `testBlankAreaUsesMoreMenuInEveryView` preserves the shared empty-space/ellipsis menu and item-menu priority in all four modes.

The broad matrix remains in OR-15. The corrected Debug app was left open for review.

Isolation avoids collision with other Zforge tasks sharing the desktop. Result counts below come from `xcresulttool`, not build success or test-source discovery.

- Baseline: `/tmp/zforge-icon-preview-baseline-unrestricted.xcresult` passed 16 distinct affected cases, zero failures/skips. Initial sandbox/signing attempts did not execute tests and are not approval.

This does not establish Developer ID, notarization or Release distribution.

Coverage includes preference defaults/persistence/inspector independence; unsupported/error fallback; cache revisions, byte/entry eviction and bounded concurrency; explicit queued cancellation; stale result exclusion; archive automatic limits and lifecycle; real PNG/JPEG, SVG via Quick Look, first-page PDF, bounded text and static HTML with relative CSS and no JavaScript.

### Failures investigated and preserved guarantees

- Initial renderer tests exposed display color-profile conversion in `NSBitmapImageRep.colorAt`. Raw pixel assertions now verify the controlled sRGB raster directly, retaining exact channel/content/alpha checks. SVG also conformed to text/image and initially showed markup; it now uses the native Quick Look content provider. Intermediate full suites (`unit-final`, `complete`, `unit-guarantee`) recorded this SVG failure; the final approved suite executes and passes it.
- Portuguese UI reproduced losing the existing selection when Command was released before the exclusive single/double-click recognizer resolved. The shared recognizer now captures modifiers from the actual mouse-down, using one native subscription and weak cell registration. Selection/multiple selection, double-click archive entry and adjacent drag/menu cases pass without changing their expected behavior.
- UI infrastructure corrections keep all scenarios/assertions: fixture-only preferences isolation, ignore restored windows, constrain/center test windows to the visible display, explicitly wait for native selection/toggle state, and close native Info/Settings through their close controls. Accessible preview/fallback identifiers belong to the file's name; decorative images remain hidden. Other app windows interrupted earlier native input; diagnostic evidence identified a separately running `io.libor.Zforge.FolderSelectionCheck` instance, which was not terminated by this task.
- Gallery's inherited mode identifier resolved to the inspector, so the old coordinate clicked metadata rather than the empty thumbnail strip. An attempted strip accessibility identifier was not exposed and was removed. The final coordinate uses the visible file row at the right edge of the window; the same blank-selection, selected-file, menu title/state and item-menu-priority assertions all pass. No menu expectation was weakened.
- Early mixed runs (`validation`, `final2`, `ui-corrected`, `ui-name`, `ui-dedicated`, `ui-gestures`, `ui-final-focus`, `ui-diagnosed`) include failures and remain diagnostic evidence, not full approval. Initial Swift compiler/runner-start failures executed zero tests. Tag live-catalog and rapid-column timing failures seen during exploratory concurrent runs were investigated; benchmark fixture encoding had blocked the main actor and now runs detached before measurement. Both established scenarios pass in the final complete suite. No tests were deleted, skipped, ignored or relaxed to obtain that result.

### Visual and measured evidence

- Inspected [English default grid](Documentation/Benchmarks/icon-previews-default.png) and [Portuguese 720 pt grid at 160%](Documentation/Benchmarks/icon-previews-portuguese-160.png), cropped to the application window. Both captures show **dark appearance**. Confirmed image aspect/fitting, document pages, fallback folder/archive/unsupported icons, shared cell alignment, selection backgrounds, native compact toolbar and localized interface. The zoomed grid remains scrollable; content below the viewport is not claimed as simultaneously visible. The launch appearance argument alone does not prove light appearance, which remains pending.
- [Isolated 200-file measurement](Documentation/Benchmarks/icon-previews.md), `/tmp/zforge-icon-preview-measured.xcresult`: one executed test, passed, zero failures/skips. Disabled baseline rendered nothing; cold service work took 337.492 ms / 0.636171 CPU s; warm 32-file work took 0.183 ms with no additional renders. Maximum service concurrency was four, raster dimension 256 px; main-actor ping p95 0.0845 ms / maximum 0.4006 ms. RSS rose from 204.80 to 220.95 MiB, peak 235.61 MiB. Raw results and reproduction are versioned. This is service work, not a frame-rate or long-session leak guarantee.
- OR-16 retains provider-specific Office/video/audio, external authorization/remote resources, huge folders, encrypted/solid archives, repeated navigation/long sessions, light appearance, VoiceOver, reduced motion, and macOS 26 runtime. No distribution/notarization or full accessibility/polish approval is inferred from these tests.

The requested product policy replaces global inclusion of hidden/outside files with visible Home/Shared discovery. `skipsHiddenItemsAndSubtrees` characterizes dot files, hidden flags, hidden parents, explicit hidden scan roots, incremental removal and preserved local tag metadata. First full implementation run fails on visibility boundary handling for temporary fixtures; recorded as an intermediate failure, not approval. The next focused run passes 25/27 cases; the scope event was not yet recognized, and the local-edit fixture's ancestry assertion was wrong. After event handling is corrected, the full run passes 226/227 distinct cases (303/304 executions), retaining only that incorrect ancestry assertion. The delivered-code full run passes 226/227 distinct cases (303/304 executions), with all tag cases passing; `columnPasteTargetsActiveColumn` fails because the destination directory exists before its recursive copy publishes `README.txt`. This is synchronization of completion, not a changed paste expectation or a timeout increase. Final suite execution follows below.

Final delivered-source validation: full suite passes **227 distinct cases/304 executions**, zero failures/skips, including the synchronized Columns paste case and all tag cases. ### Failed rounds and repairs

- Official MCP SDK 0.11.0 fails Xcode 27 concurrency checking in NetworkTransport; official 0.12.0 includes the repair. No dependency sources or checks were weakened.
- Initial `symlinkEscape` fails because Foundation does not resolve a symlink parent when the final output is nonexistent. Resolving the nearest existing ancestor fixes the defect; the same expected denial passes afterward.
- Initial localized picker queries select a Touch Bar button or wrongly expect a Dialog. The observed `open-panel` Window and Escape preserve both language scenarios and the original grant/configuration expectations.
- General-sample `updateAppShortcutParameters()` produces a native refresh error and does not establish Mac discovery. Apple HIG's Mac constraint required removing that unsupported provider/call. Installing build 2 makes all four actions visible; 104 historical registered build-1 copies were not removed.
- Real saved native integrity initially fails despite its explicit folder grant. ### Resource measurements and failed gates

The predeclared workload is 10,000 visible direct children / 100 archives, with name-query <=100 ms, snapshot <=250 ms and initial native 100-entity donation <=2 s. Initial candidates fail the gates: isolated name/snapshot 120.599/550.562 ms, then 132.792/127.383 ms after classification filtering. Test-host production-index isolation produces 51.447/47.007 ms. The directory-alias change subsequently adds unnecessary URL construction for all 10,000 children: the concurrent probe build round measures 131.034/146.968 ms and the next complete round measures 107.477/113.281 ms. The implementation now constructs presented URLs only after archive filtering; final complete regression measures **49.020 ms name / 51.102 ms snapshot**, preserving both thresholds and the 100-result expectations. Snapshot CPU is 0.041807 user / 0.019972 system seconds; whole-test-host peak RSS is 261,734,400 bytes. CPU/RSS are not an isolated index-memory delta or a matched baseline improvement claim.

Installed signed build 14 initial publication of 100 synthetic entities is **150.060 ms** (PID 32741, 11:12:33.901); event publication in the previous running instance is 114.896 ms, a separate sample. Only 97 exclusively created, hash-checked benchmark additions are removed; native reconciliation returns to three entities in 26.525 ms. A 10.015 s quiet sample of the whole Recents/Gallery application records 1.12 CPU seconds and unchanged 172,867,584-byte RSS. There is no archive reconciliation between that launch and fixture cleanup. This establishes no periodic rescan in the recorded interval, not exclusive index idle CPU or incremental memory acceptance. Larger archive populations, unavailable/external volumes, full frame pacing and macOS 26 runtime remain pending.

## ZF-43 Siri in-app archive search — 2026-10-13

- New and adjacent suites (`ArchiveSearchIntentTests`, `SiriArchiveIntentTests`, `FileArchiveIntentTests`, `LocalizationTests`): **50 tests in 4 suites passed**, zero failures/skips, `/tmp/ZforgeSearch24Unit2.xcresult`. The first run failed one new expectation because the test stub itself retained the lease; the stub now hands the lease over once, with the expectation unchanged.
- Full unit regression (`ZforgeTests`), `/tmp/ZforgeSearch25Regression.xcresult`: **361 tests, 359 passed, 2 failed**, zero skipped. Both failures are in `TagCatalogTests`, which this change does not touch:
  - “Native menu circles share the current selection's applied tag state” also fails on the unchanged branch head `ffee8fb` (`/tmp/ZforgeBaselineAITags.xcresult`) — **preexisting**.
  - “A partial index retries its inaccessible subtree without rescanning accessible items” is **intermittent**: it passed on the unchanged baseline once and in one of two isolated runs of the changed tree (`/tmp/ZforgeSearch25Tags1.xcresult` pass, `Tags2` fail). Not yet shown to be preexisting; investigate before integration. No assertion was changed.
- Tag failures resolved, 2026-10-13:
  - “Native menu circles…” depended on the Mac's own Finder tags: the quick-tag row shows the first seven catalog tags alphabetically, and this Mac's Portuguese tags (Amarelo, ATI, Azul, Cinza, …) pushed the fixture's `Red` out. The feature itself behaves correctly. `FinderTagQuickMenu.append`/`tags(for:)` now accept the catalog tags (defaulting to the shared catalog); the test checks the circle states against a fixed `Blue`/`Red` catalog, plus unapplied `Blue`.
  - “A partial index retries…” failed 10 of 60 repetitions (`/tmp/ZforgeTagRetry2.xcresult`), scanning `Denied` twice and sometimes `B`. Changes made just before an FSEvents stream starts can arrive after it with later IDs (confirmed with a standalone stream; `FSEventStreamFlushAsync` returns 0), so the first checkpoint may predate the fixture's setup events and the reopened index replays them. **Implementation fix:** history replayed inside a restored deferred retry scope is now ignored like a cold root's, because the retry scan starts after the journal and already reflects it; after the fix, 3 of 60 failed and only on `B` (`/tmp/ZforgeTagRetry3.xcresult`). The remaining `B` rescan is a correct replay of unsettled setup events, so the test now waits for a hidden marker's FSEvents delivery before indexing.
  - Both tests: 200 of 200 repetitions passed (`/tmp/ZforgeTagRetry4.xcresult`). Full unit regression `/tmp/ZforgeTagFull.xcresult`: **362 tests passed**, zero failures or skips.
- Probe controls: 10 tests in 3 suites passed with `PROBE_COMPATIBILITY_SCHEMA PROBE_SEARCH_IN_APP`, `/tmp/ZforgeProbeSearch9Unit2.xcresult`.
- Launch window reuse (build 26): new case `launchWindowReuse` covers single reuse, other windows, the grace period, a closed launch window and later default windows; `ArchiveSearchIntentTests` and `SiriArchiveIntentTests` pass, 33 tests, zero failures. The interaction monitor and SwiftUI window replacement are covered only by native acceptance.

New contracts (62 − 57 = 5 tests, 19 executions): exact-name `listxattr` prefilter (including similar names, a missing path and raw list parsing); oversized name lists keep the direct read and still discover tags; bulk package pre-check (dotless bundle-bit package excluded, plain and dotted folders traversed, native equals Foundation fallback); cooperative duty (blocked time is not CPU work, bounded 50 ms overshoot credit, CPU-bound work measured); 15 path-component normalization cases. **FileOperationServiceTests QoS warning investigated:** the candidate xcresult attributes the single runtime warning ("[Internal] Thread running at User-interactive quality-of-service class waiting on a lower QoS thread running at Default…") to `FileOperationServiceTests.newFolderStartsRenaming()`, `FileOperationServiceTests.swift` line 144 — the teardown that detaches the `NSHostingController` and closes a window with an active rename field editor. The `[Internal]` prefix marks a wait inside system frameworks; no tag discovery, scanner or Zforge background worker is involved (discovery runs at utility priority and never blocks the main thread). Unchanged test fixture; not fixed in this round.

Measurements and limits: [tag-first-pass-optimization.md](Documentation/Benchmarks/tag-first-pass-optimization.md). Not covered: the signed application's cold first pass and 10,000-record launch (protected container index), full four-mode visual review (no UI change is made), macOS 26 runtime.

### CPU duty cycle removed by user decision — 2026-10-24

The user changed the requirement ("now that we've mostly solved the cost of the operation, remove the CPU usage limit and run the test again"). `TaggedFileScanner.Budget.pause(Duration)` becomes `yieldSlice()` (default: `Task.yield()` plus cancellation check); `CooperativeDuty` becomes `WorkSlice` with the same 128-item/8 ms boundaries. Test changes and their justification:

- `scannerBoundsBatchesAndYields`: the assertion `pauses.allSatisfy { $0 > .zero }` encoded the 20% duty contract and is removed **because the user removed that requirement**. Every other assertion is kept: tagged batches at most 128, all 257 records, at least three slice boundaries for tagged and untagged trees, and at least three publications. The probe now counts yields instead of pause durations.
- `canceledVisibilityBatchDoesNotPublishPartialRecords`: the cancellation gate moves from the pause hook to the yield hook. The scenario and assertions are unchanged (cancel at the first boundary, no partial result, exactly one boundary reached).
- This round's own `cooperativeDutyMeasuresCPUAndCreditsOvershoot` tested the removed component and is replaced by `sliceBoundariesYieldAndObserveCancellation`: item/time slice limits, counter reset and cancellation observed at the default boundary.
- `TagDiscoveryWorkProfile.swift` (added earlier this round) is deleted; `TagDiscoveryColdProfile.swift` records yields; `TagDiscoveryAppPathProfile.swift` adds main-actor heartbeat, per-second CPU and thermal-state reporting.

| Executed result | Distinct tests | Executions | Outcome |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| TagCatalogTests, `/private/tmp/zf41r/nocap-tags.xcresult` | 62 | 90 | All pass, zero skips. |
| Full unit suite, `/private/tmp/zf41r/nocap-unit.xcresult` | 367 | 557 | All pass, zero failures/skips. The archive discovery benchmark that failed under load in the capped run passes here. The FileOperationServiceTests QoS diagnostic remains. |
| Differential equivalence, `/private/tmp/zf41r/equivalence-nocap` | 76 checkpoints | 152 comparisons + fresh rescans | All pass. |

Tag UI cases were not re-run after this change: it alters no UI, and the three cases already fail 2 of 3 identically on unchanged `HEAD` (D-52). Performance and responsiveness evidence: [benchmark](Documentation/Benchmarks/tag-first-pass-optimization.md#user-decision-cpu-duty-cycle-removed--2026-10-24).

### Concurrent traversal experiment — 2026-10-24

New contracts (3 tests, 5 executions): `concurrentTraversalMatchesSingleWorker` (2, 4 and 8 workers; an uneven tree with a 24-level chain, a 700-file directory, unequal branches, Unicode names including a leading combining mark, a tagged folder, file and directory links, dot-hidden and `UF_HIDDEN` folders, a package and an unreadable folder; five repetitions per count compare records, inspected and metadata-read counts and deferred sets with one worker, check batches stay at most 128, and repeat with the Foundation fallback); `canceledConcurrentTraversalStops` (four gated workers, cancellation mid-scan: interrupted result, no deferred claim, no publication after return, only fixture records published); `catalogWithConcurrentWorkersMatchesSingleWorker` (catalog cold discovery and checkpoint with four workers). The one-worker path is refactored into shared seed and entry helpers with unchanged behavior; existing tests are not modified in this step.

| Executed result (screen locked, unrelated Godot load) | Distinct tests | Executions | Outcome |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |

| Unchanged `HEAD` TagCatalogTests, same locked session, `base-tags-locked.xcresult` | 57 | 71 | 50 / 64 pass; the same seven D-49 contracts fail. Earlier the same day, unlocked, the same build passed 57 / 71. |
| Candidate full unit suite, `par-unit.xcresult` | 370 | 562 | 360 / 550 pass. Failures: the eight checkpoint loads above; `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` (146.0 ms vs 100 ms under load); `updatesGlobalCatalogueLive`, where one 100 ms poll took about 17 s in the locked, loaded session. |

These failures come from the locked session: protected checkpoints cannot be reopened (D-49 root cause), and the session was heavily loaded. They are not attributed to the experiment, whose default remains one worker. **Superseded:** the concurrent code was later removed by user decision. The blocked reruns were performed unlocked on the final one-worker source (next section).

### Decisions applied and unlocked verification — 2026-10-24

**Multi-worker rejected by the user; the default and only mode is one worker.** Removed: `Budget.workers`, the shared-directory-stack traversal (`ConcurrentTraversal`, seed/entry helpers), `FinderTagCatalog(scanBudget:)`, the `ZF_TAG_WORKERS` benchmark switch and the three concurrent tests with their fixture helper. The scanner is restored to its previous simpler single-worker structure; `TaggedFileScanner.swift` and `FinderTagCatalog.swift` are byte-identical (SHA-256 `45d37557…`, `731e8838…`) to the one-worker build measured and tested before the experiment. **D-49:** the user keeps the index protection class. Precondition: the screen must be unlocked when running the tag tests, the differential harness and the D-43 benchmark; in a locked session the seven checkpoint contracts fail for that reason only, and they are not skipped or loosened.

Runs from `/private/tmp/zf41r/final-src`, under `caffeinate -dimsu`. A guard confirmed the session was unlocked before and after every run and recorded unrelated load (a Godot workload that intermittently used up to 5.6 cores, plus Spotlight `mds`/`mds_stores`).

## ZF-47 — Archive navigation limits as sliders — 2026-10-29

- **Specification change (user request):** the depth and root size steppers became sliders, and the root size range went from 1–5 GB to 256 MB–16 GB (decimal bytes, D-11 preserved). Two existing expectations were changed for this reason only: `nestedArchivePolicy` now clamps to 16 GB (was 5 GB) and also checks the new lower bounds, and `archivePreferences` uses `archiveRootSizeLimit` in bytes with a 16 GB ceiling (was `archiveRootSizeLimitGB` with 5). The default (5 levels, 1 GB) and the strict `<` comparison are unchanged.
- **New unit coverage:** `legacyArchiveRootSizeLimit` (whole-GB setting migrates to decimal bytes) and `SettingsSliderTests` (11 tests, 13 executions): steps per doubling (384 → 392/376 MB, 992 MB → 1 GB, 1 GB → 1.03125 GB, 16 GB end), round values and Option jumps, tick alignment, a 40,001-sample drag sweep that reaches all 192 steps in order (no step hidden by a hold), holds around every round value, the linear depth scale, tick-label layout (under the ticks, inside the slider, no overlap at 520 pt), the drag action snapping the knob, arrow/Option/Up/End keys, VoiceOver value and increment/decrement, and the Settings section bound to the preferences with snapshots in light, dark and dark pt-BR.
- **Defects found by these tests before completion:** (1) a floating-point gap at the edge of each hold sent the value back to 256 MB (`value(at:)` rewritten so every position resolves to a hold or the steps before it); (2) tick labels clipped their last glyph at their exact intrinsic width (visible in the first recorded snapshot; now 2 pt wider). The first snapshot references were discarded and recorded again after the fix; the snapshot is taken before the test moves the sliders, because the native knob animates value changes. After that, 6 consecutive runs of the suite passed.
- **Unit plan:** 471 tests; all new and changed tests passed. Two failures outside this change: `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests` "A bounded 10000-child workload…" (mean above 150 ms and file time above 250 ms under full-plan load) and `TagCatalogTests` "File events update tags and results without navigating or refreshing" (`TagCatalogTests.swift:1626`). Both suites then passed in isolation twice (64/64). A concurrent agent session was running its own Unit plan on the same machine at the same time.
- **UI (not run):** `SettingsUITests.testArchiveSizeLimitSliderHoldsRoundValuesWhileDragging` (new: a 2 pt drag stays on 1 GB, an 8 pt drag reaches 1.06 GB) and the other 14 mapped UI tests could not start: a Debug Zforge launched from Xcode at 21:14 was held by the debugger (state `SX`), and neither XCTest, SIGTERM nor SIGKILL could end it. **Pending.**
- **Map:** the new files are mapped to the new UI test. `affected-tests.py --check` reports one problem from separate uncommitted work: `testListHeaderDividerResizesAndHeaderBodyReorders` is reached by no rule.
- **Visual review:** the three snapshots were viewed (labels complete, values in the language's format). Not seen yet in the running app: accent fill in a key window, drag feel, haptics, VoiceOver.
- **Correction after the user's review in the running app (same day):** (1) the depth labels were not centred on their marks: they followed `rectOfTickMark(at:)`, which on macOS 26 reports marks at the very ends of the slider, while the marks and knob centre are drawn inset by half the knob (10 pt), measured from a rendered `NSSlider` (2 pt dots, tertiary-label colour, at the bottom of the 16 pt slider). The earlier test used `rectOfTickMark` as its oracle, so it passed while the drawing was wrong. (2) The size slider lacked the unlabelled midpoint marks from the sketch. Fix: labels and the new `MinorTickMarksView` dots use `ScaleSliderView.trackX(at:)` (knob-width inset); the midpoint marks sit behind the slider so the knob covers them like the native ones. New test `labelsFollowDrawnTickMarks` (light and dark) reads the marks from the rendered view and checks every drawn mark (7 + 6 on the size slider, 10 on depth) and every unclamped label against them; with the old `rectOfTickMark` placement it fails (label offsets above 1 pt), with the fix it passes. The three snapshots were re-recorded with `ZFORGE_RECORD_SNAPSHOTS=1` for this approved visual change and viewed; then 4 consecutive runs of `SettingsSliderTests` (12 tests) passed.

## D-60 — List header divider drags moved columns — 2026-10-29

- **Report:** the user's recording shows a drag on the Name | Date Modified divider resizing a few points and then moving the column. Cause in DIVERGENCES D-60: the resize handle was inside the header cell's `onDrag` reorder source and its sort button.
- **UI:** `ZforgeUITests.testListHeaderDividerResizesAndHeaderBodyReorders` (List, Recents fixture, isolated preferences suite) drags the Name divider 60 pt right and the Date Modified divider 40 pt right, checking that the next header follows the pointer (±2 pt) and the column order is unchanged, then drags the Size header body onto Date Modified and checks the reorder. Without the fix it fails: the divider moved 4.5 of 60 pt (`/tmp/zf-ui-before2.xcresult`); with it it passes (`/tmp/zf-ui-after.xcresult`). A first draft dragged Name left, which cannot pass because the fixture's Name column is already at its 168 pt minimum; it was corrected before the fix was judged. Mapped in `ui-impact.json` to `ListBrowserView.swift` and `BrowserLayout.swift`. Why UI: the defect is which gesture receives real mouse events (AppKit handle vs. SwiftUI drag session), which offscreen layers do not deliver.
- **Unit plan (2026-10-29):** 471 tests, 469 passed (`/tmp/zf-header-unit.xcresult`). Failures: `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` (mean 166 ms, limit 150 ms) and `TagCatalogTests` "File events update tags and results without navigating or refreshing" (initial discovery waited 22 s), both the known intermittent failures recorded above, under concurrent builds from another session; unrelated to the header.
- **Selected UI tests (2026-10-29):** the 16 tests mapped to `ListBrowserView.swift` and `BrowserLayout.swift`: 15 passed (`/tmp/zf-header-ui.xcresult`). `testArchivePromisesPublishIntoFinderBeforeRemovingOriginal` failed once at the Option-drag's "Modify the Original Archive?" confirmation (line 650; the plain copy drop before it succeeded) and passed when rerun alone (`/tmp/zf-header-finder.xcresult`). Recorded as intermittent; its row drag path is not touched by this change; not investigated further.
- **Limits:** visual review in the user's own build is pending; the cursor shape over the divider and the zoom levels other than 100 % were not checked in this run.

## Archive discovery benchmark — test tolerance — 2026-10-28

- **Decision (user, 2026-10-28):** the feature target for name discovery stays at 100 ms; `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` now runs the name query three times (each must return the 100 archives) and accepts a mean of at most 150 ms. Snapshot and file-association limits (250 ms) are unchanged. Recorded in `Documentation/Requirements/AGENT-INTEGRATION-PLAN.md`.

## Settings — drag to reorder List columns — 2026-10-27

The user asked to reorder the default List columns by dragging instead of one-step arrow buttons.
- **Correction, 2026-10-28:** the user found that dragging did nothing. The first version used `ForEach.onMove`, which had no effect in the Settings `Form`; an `onDrag`/drop-delegate version also never reordered. Rows now use a `DragGesture` on a `line.3.horizontal` handle at the leading edge (open/closed grab pointer). The column moves live, one place per row pitch travelled from where the drag started (`ListColumnReorder.targetIndex`, row pitch read from the rows' frames so a scrolled Form does not matter); Name and hidden rows keep an empty slot of the same width.
- **Unit:** `ListColumnOrderTests` (5 tests): moves onto another row in both directions, persistence, Name stays first, the translation-to-place mapping (half-row threshold, clamping at Name and the last visible column, no move without measured rows) and the one-step accessibility moves. Passed.
- **UI:** `SettingsUITests.testListColumnsReorderByDragging` scrolls the List Columns section into view, drags Kind's handle above Date Modified and checks the rows and the List header order. With the handle's gesture disabled it fails (order unchanged, header order unchanged), and it passes with the gesture; its earlier failures against the `onDrag` version are not evidence, because two test defects were found and fixed: the section was below the fold (events went outside the rows), and `press(forDuration:thenDragTo:withVelocity:thenHoldForDuration:)` delivered no mouse events to the app on macOS (verified with a temporary event log); it now uses `click(forDuration:thenDragTo:)` like the other drag tests. Passed with the final `DragGesture` handle.
- **Selected run (2026-10-28):** `Scripts/affected-tests.py` selection, 21 UI tests, all passed (802 s). Full unit plan: 459 tests, 458 passed; `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` failed under machine load as before. In an earlier run of the same plan `TagCatalogTests` "File events update tags and results without navigating or refreshing" failed once and passed in isolation (63/63); recorded as intermittent, not investigated further here.
- **UI infrastructure fixes in the same run (scenarios and assertions unchanged):** `testCompactToolbarMenusOpenFromTheirToolbarButtons`, `testArchiveCopyIntoAnotherZforgeWindow` and `testArchivePromisesPublishIntoFinderBeforeRemovingOriginal` launched without a browser window when the previous test left none; they now use the shared `launchWithBrowserWindow` helper (renamed from `launchArchiveFixture`). The Finder promise test dropped onto a point covered by one of the user's own Finder windows and copied `Payload.txt` into `~/Pictures` (the stray file was verified as the fixture's and removed). It now anchors the Zforge window to the leading edge (`--ui-window-leading`, a UI-test launch argument next to `--ui-window-width`) and only drops on a point where the window server reports the test's own Finder window frontmost (`CGWindowListCopyWindowInfo`), failing without dropping otherwise.
- **Visual review:** the Settings capture after the drag shows the handles on the leading edge, empty slots on Name and hidden rows, and aligned titles. Not yet checked by the user in their own build.
- **Localization:** the Settings caption now says to drag, and the handle's help "Drag to reorder" is new, both in the 21 languages.

## ZF-45 — Test layers, selection and XCUITest migration (RNF-Q08) — 2026-10-27

### Method

Six areas were migrated in isolated worktrees.
- **Removal rule:** a UI test, or one of its assertions, left XCUITest only after an offscreen test covering it passed.
- **Regression detection:** each new characterization test was shown to catch a regression. The production behavior was broken on purpose, the test failed for the expected reason, and the code was restored and checked with `git diff`. About 45 such breaks were recorded; each area's report lists them.
- **No UI runs:** UI tests were compiled but not run (`build-for-testing -testPlan UI-Full` succeeded on the merged tree).

### Production changes

The production changes are behavior-preserving extractions for testability:
- `canOpenSelection` and `openSelection()` on `FileBrowserState`;
- `KeyboardShortcutCatalog.sections(matching:locale:)`;
- `BrowserToolbarLayout.OverflowMenuContents`;
- `ArchiveDropDestinationView.operation(for:option:sourceMask:)` and `perform(pasteboard:operation:)`;
- `CompressionSaveDialog.makePanel()`, plus `CompressionSettingsSection` extracted from Settings;
- an optional `catalogTags:` parameter on the menu builders;
- fixture service hooks (`itemsGate:`, `includesArchiveSessionCopies:`);
- a thumbnail callback on `IconPreviewView`;
- the Info window title function.

No visible string changed.

### Results

- **Unit plan on the merged tree:** 449 tests, 446 passed, 3 failed, 0 skipped (`/tmp/zf45-unit.xcresult`). The full plan ran while Spotlight was indexing (load average about 15–20).
- **`ZforgeTests.rapidColumnArchiveSelectionPublishesOnlyCurrentArchive` and `TagCatalogTests.updatesGlobalCatalogueLive`:** both passed when rerun alone (`/tmp/zf45-rerun2.xcresult`). These are the known load-sensitive cases.
- **`ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` (100 ms budget):** failed at 120–176 ms. It fails the same way on unmodified HEAD under the same load (`/tmp/zf45-base-perf1.xcresult`, `/tmp/zf45-base-perf2.xcresult`), so it is pre-existing and depends on the environment. It is unchanged; it needs a rerun on an idle machine.
- **First snapshot test:** `MenuSymbolSnapshotTests.finderSymbolStrip` (light and dark) passes unchanged and failed when the Open symbol was swapped.
- **`Scripts/affected-tests.py --check`:** 22 UI tests, 19 rules, 0 problems. Sample selections:
  - `CompressionSaveDialog.swift` → 1 UI test.
  - Documentation plus unit tests → none.
  - An unmapped view → `UI-Smoke`, with a warning.

- **Kept UI tests, 2026-10-27 (after the D-58 fix):** full `UI-Full` run, 22 tests in 825 s: 16 passed, 6 failed (`/tmp/zf58-ui.xcresult`). The machine was under heavy load from unrelated processes (load average 15, rising to 84–204 during the reruns). Triage:
  - `testMCPContextualPermissionCancellationAndDisconnect`, `testArchiveCopyIntoAnotherZforgeWindow` and `testCompactToolbarMenusOpenFromTheirToolbarButtons` timed out at launch or on the first panel. They passed unchanged when rerun (`/tmp/zf58-ui-rerun.xcresult`). Load-sensitive; not reproduced.
  - `testLocalDragsOptionCopyAndConfirmAdditionToArchive` (merged by ZF-45): after the archive was expanded, two `Local.txt` rows existed and the Option-drag picked the archive entry, which Option moves out of the archive ("Modify the Original Archive?"). The test now collapses the archive first, restoring the old copy test's starting state and its expected count of 2. Passed (`/tmp/zf58-ui-rerun.xcresult`).
  - `testLanguageSelectionUpdatesOpenMenuAndPersists`: pre-existing (the line is unchanged from HEAD). It waited for a window identifier, `browser-main-window`, that no longer exists, and launches can restore no window. It now waits for a `browser-` window and opens one with ⌘N when none appears, as the other fixture launches do, at launch and after relaunch (File > Open takes its language from the focused window). Passed (`/tmp/zf58-ui-rerun4.xcresult`).
  - `testArchivePromisesPublishIntoFinderBeforeRemovingOriginal`: pre-existing choreography (unchanged from HEAD). It moves Finder by pressing its toolbar 300 pt from the left; with this Mac's Finder sidebar width that point is the back/forward control, so Finder stayed under Zforge and the drop landed on Zforge (failed 3 of 3). The drop point is now on Finder's content where Zforge does not cover it, with a precondition that fails clearly otherwise. Assertions are unchanged. Passed (`/tmp/zf58-ui-rerun5.xcresult`).
- **Unit plan after the D-58 fix:** 450 tests, 449 passed; `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` failed at 118–144 ms under the same load (`/tmp/zf58-unit.xcresult`).

### Limits

- **No single clean sweep:** every kept UI test has passed, but across one full run and four targeted reruns, not in one uninterrupted `UI-Full` run. The machine was heavily loaded throughout.
- **Reduced Portuguese coverage:** Portuguese automation text is now checked only against the String Catalog.
- **Machine-dependent snapshots:** they depend on this Mac's scale, accent color and system icons.
- **Recording from a worktree:** the sandboxed test host could not write references inside `/tmp` worktrees. The agents imported them from result-bundle attachments. Recording from the main checkout works.
- **Collateral `pkill`:** during the migration one agent ran a `pkill` that matched any Zforge process, which may have ended other test hosts or the user's own Zforge at about 15:03.

Snapshot `snapshot` (light/dark × active/inactive, 4 references under `ZforgeSnapshots/SidebarIconTintTests/`) renders a `.sidebar` List with the neutral role set on the Locations section, as in `BrowserSidebar`, so it also proves that the section environment reaches its rows; tag dots stay colored.

## ZF-49 — Sidebar item menu with Finder parity (Favorites) — 2026-10-29

- **Request:** Favorites' context menu offered only Remove from Sidebar; the user asked for Finder's menu, order and grouping (capture): Open in New Tab, Show in Enclosing Folder │ Remove from Sidebar │ Get Info, Add to Dock.
- **Unit (new, `SidebarItemMenuTests`, 7 tests / 9 cases):** order, titles and separators; stable identifiers; a symbol on every item; enablement (Add to Dock disabled in the sandbox, Show in Enclosing Folder disabled for `/`); a non-removable destination omits Remove and its separator; each enabled item runs exactly its own command; groups inserted by other sections keep the shared order; titles in pt-BR, de and ja.
- **Unit (new, `SidebarItemMenuActionTests`, 6 tests, real `FileSystemService` on temporary folders):** Show in Enclosing Folder opens the parent in the same browser with the destination selected; without access it asks for the parent and leaves the view unchanged; Open in New Tab calls this window's tab opener (not the new-window opener); without access it asks first and opens the tab only after authorization; Get Info for a missing destination reports an error and opens no info window; Remove from Sidebar unpins only that favorite, keeps the folder and restores content focus.
- **Results:** derived data `/tmp/zforge-dd-task3`. The first three `Unit` runs stopped at compilation on in-progress edits from the parallel sidebar threads: a `leaveUnmountedVolume` switch missing `.computer`, `SidebarLocationMenuTests` racing its `computerMenu` definition, and imports/isolation in `ComputerLocationTests`. None of these were in this change's files. Fourth run (`/tmp/zforge-task3-unit4.xcresult`): 532 tests, 526 passed, 6 failed, 0 skipped. Failures: (1) this change's Get Info test compared the global `NSApp.windows.count`, which other suites change in parallel. That oracle was replaced by a check for an info window titled with the missing item's name: the scenario is unchanged and the check is just as strict. (2) Known load-dependent `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren`. (3) Known intermittent `TagCatalogTests.updatesGlobalCatalogueLive`. (4) `rapidColumnArchiveSelectionPublishesOnlyCurrentArchive`, which passes alone (`/tmp/zforge-task3-unit6.xcresult`); it is load-dependent and unrelated to the sidebar. (5–6) ZF-52's `ComputerLocationTests` snapshot, light and dark, belonging to the parallel thread, likely a first recording. Targeted re-run of both new suites (`/tmp/zforge-task3-unit5.xcresult`): 13 of 13 passed. `Scripts/affected-tests.py --check`: 0 problems.

The real right-click presentation of the SwiftUI `.contextMenu` and attachment of the new tab to the source window are only provable in the running app. They are pending, along with an in-app look (light/dark, German, an inactive window) and a decision on Add to Dock (OR-22).

## ZF-50 — Inline eject button for ejectable volumes in Locations — 2026-10-29

- **Contract:** `CURRENT-STATE.md` (sidebar, “Inline eject button like Finder”) and `UI-UX-GUIDE.md` (sidebar). One handler, `FileBrowserState.ejectVolume(at:name:)`, serves the button, File ▸ Eject and the sidebar Eject menu item.
- **New offscreen coverage** (`ZforgeTests/SidebarVolumeEjectTests.swift`, 11 tests / 18 executions): the ejectability table (boot volume, USB flash drive, external SSD, disk image, network share, internal partition, root reporting ejectable); the mounted boot volume is listed as not ejectable; busy classification (POSIX `EBUSY`, wrapped underlying error, `OSStatus` −47, Disk Arbitration busy; `EPERM` and a Cocoa code 16 are not busy); volume membership by path (`/Volumes/Ventoy 2` and `VentoyData` are not on `Ventoy`); ejecting the browsed volume ejects once and moves to the computer level without an alert; ejecting another volume keeps the location; a busy failure keeps the location and shows the localized in-use alert; another failure keeps the system reason; a repeated request during an in-flight eject is ignored while `ejectingVolumeURLs` reports it; an unmount observed from outside moves only windows on that volume. Snapshots `ZforgeSnapshots/SidebarVolumeEjectTests/` (idle and drop-target rows, light and dark) were viewed: secondary ⏏ at the trailing edge, white over the accent fill. The eject itself is injected (`volumeEjector`); no test ejects a real disk.
- **Real-system check of the busy error:** a throwaway APFS disk image with an open file made `NSWorkspace.unmountAndEjectDevice(at:)` throw `NSOSStatusErrorDomain` −47 (system text “OSStatus error -47.”, hence the dedicated localized message); after closing the file the same call ejected it. The image reported ejectable and removable, so it gets the button.
- **Unit plan, full run** (`/tmp/zforge-dd-task2`, while other agent threads edited and tested the same checkout): 532 tests in 56 suites, 2 failing tests / 3 issues, none in this change's suite. `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests` (10,000-child timing budget; the file has the user's own uncommitted edits) also fails alone; “Rapid column archive selection cancels the previous load before opening the next” timed out under load and passes alone. Neither exercises the eject code. An earlier run was blocked by another thread's then-uncompilable `ComputerLocationTests.swift`; a copy of the checkout without that file ran 519 tests with the same unrelated failures plus first-recording issues.
- **`Scripts/affected-tests.py --check`:** 25 UI tests, 20 rules, 0 problems (`Zforge/Views/SidebarEjectButton.swift` added to the Sidebar rule).
- **Not run here:** UI tests (concurrent threads share the screen; the parent thread runs one consolidated pass). Selection for this change's files: `testSettingsProvidesLocalizedAgentSetupAndNativeCancellation`, `testSettingsToggleUpdatesTheOpenGridAndKeepsSelection`, `testLanguageSelectionUpdatesOpenMenuAndPersists`, `testArchiveDragCopiesIntoSidebarFolder`, `testArchiveInternalCopyConfirmationKeepsHistoryAndAncestorColumns`, `testBlankAreaUsesMoreMenuInEveryView`, `testColumnPreviewRefitsAfterUserScrollsLeft`, `testCompactToolbarMenusOpenFromTheirToolbarButtons`, `testFileOpenNavigatesSelectedFolderAndArchiveInColumnsMode`, `testKeyboardShortcutHelpOpensAndFilters`, `testListKeyboardZoomAndArrowNavigationAfterChangingView`, `testLocalDragsOptionCopyAndConfirmAdditionToArchive`, `testNewFolderWithSelectionMenuAndShortcutInListMode`, `testTagEditorIsSharedByToolbarAndContextMenu`, `testViewModeIsPerWindowAndLastClosedWindowRestoresIt`. No new UI test: the click reaching the overlaid button is the only part offscreen layers cannot prove, and a UI test would need a real ejectable volume.
- **Pending:** in-app check with a real removable drive (click and hover, selected row, inactive window, light/dark, long language, narrow sidebar, spinner during a slow eject); the inline button for Shared network volumes; whether browsing inside an archive stored on the volume keeps it busy.

Requirement change: `SidebarVolumeMenuCommand.eraseDisk`, `FileBrowserState.openDiskUtilityToErase()` and the "Erase Disk…" / "Disk Utility could not be opened." catalog keys were deleted.

- **Correction (same day):** the user clarified that Finder grays **every** name in an inactive window; the intended fix was the opposite of the primary-name change above. New contract: the shared `sidebarLabelStyle` (primary or accent while active, `secondaryLabelColor` while inactive, white on a drop target) is applied to every row name — destinations, the computer row, `SidebarActionRow` (Recents, Remote) and tags. The fixture's destination and tag rows use the same modifier. The four references will be re-recorded as an approved visual change and viewed.

Spotlight (This Mac) latency was not measured (depends on the user's index; pending, OR-24).
