## Volume rename under the App Sandbox — 2026-10-30

- **Report:** renaming the USB drive “Ventoy” (sidebar Rename “Name” and the content views' inline rename) failed with “You don’t have permission to save the file “Ventoy” in the folder “Volumes”.”
- **Reproduction before the fix (sandboxed test host, temporary probe removed afterwards):** on disposable exFAT, HFS+ and FAT32 disk images (`hdiutil` outside the sandbox, identity checked through Disk Arbitration as model “Disk Image”, protocol “Virtual Interface”), `URLResourceValues.volumeName` failed with Cocoa error 513 and the same message, and `DADiskRename` returned `kDAReturnNotPrivileged` (0xF8DA0009) — with no grant and with an app-created bookmark of the mount point (read-only; writing a file was still denied). With Zforge's stored `/` grant active (the container holds grants for `/` and the home folder), both APIs renamed all three images; exFAT and FAT32 refused a name with `:` and `/` with 0xC016 (`EINVAL`). diskarbitrationd's binary names a client sandbox check (`file-write-unlink`). `access(W_OK)` on `/Volumes/Ventoy` (read-only check, the drive was not modified) changes from denied to allowed with the `/` grant, as for the images.
- **Not reproduced / open (OR-25):** Ventoy is mounted by root, the images by the user. For a non-owner Disk Arbitration also evaluates `system.volume.removable.rename`; requested directly by the sandboxed host it is denied (-60005), outside the sandbox granted (0). Whether that blocks the rename of Ventoy could not be tested without renaming the user's drive.
- **Fix:** `renameVolume(at:from:to:)` keeps its validation, activates the grant covering the volume (`activateSidebarGrant`), asks for access with the existing panel when there is none (`AuthorizationPurpose.renameVolume`, renaming after `authorize`), and renames through the injected `VolumeRenaming` (`DiskArbitrationVolumeRenamer`, `DADiskRename` on its own queue). `VolumeRenameFailure(status:)` maps Disk Arbitration and `unix_err(errno)` statuses; each failure has a localized message (7 new strings in all 21 languages).
- **Tests (`ZforgeTests/VolumeRenameTests.swift`):** suite “Volume rename” (7 tests; parameterized cases: 7 messages, 19 status mappings): success with the trimmed name; invalid and unchanged names never reach the renamer; each refusal's English message; status mapping; no grant → access panel requested, no rename, then rename after `authorize`; content rename field closes on success and keeps the typed name on failure; a content rename waiting for access closes after the granted rename. Suite “Volume rename on a disk image”: creates, verifies and renames its own exFAT image (refused without a write grant, invalid name, renamed with a grant).
- **Results:** derived data `/tmp/zforge-dd-rename`; full `Unit` plan (`/tmp/zforge-rename-unit.log`, `/tmp/zforge-rename-unit-1793356949.xcresult`): 578 tests in 63 suites, 1 issue — the known `rapidColumnArchiveSelectionPublishesOnlyCurrentArchive` (D-28). “Volume rename” and “Sidebar Locations item menu” passed. **Skipped:** “Volume rename on a disk image” cancelled itself because `hdiutil create` exits 1 inside the sandboxed host; the live Disk Arbitration evidence is the probe above.
- **Not executed:** UI tests (they take over the screen); `Scripts/affected-tests.py` selects `UI-Smoke` plus the mapped rules for this checkout. No in-app rename of a real drive or visual check of the access panel flow.

## Volume rename removed (OR-25 resolved) — 2026-10-30

- **User decision (contract change):** remove Rename for volumes entirely. In the running sandboxed app, renaming “Ventoy” (mounted by root) failed with “You don’t have permission to rename “Ventoy”.”: Disk Arbitration requires `system.volume.removable.rename` for volumes the user doesn't own, which the App Sandbox denies. The user chose removal over hiding it per volume, a helper, or an entitlement exception (as for OR-22 and OR-23).
- **Changes:** the Locations menu no longer has Rename “Name” (`SidebarVolumeMenuCommand` is only `.eject`; `SidebarLocationMenu.commands(for:canEject:)`); deleted `SidebarVolumeNameField`, `SidebarState.renamingVolumeURL`/`volumeRenameDraft`/`beginRenamingVolume`/`endRenamingVolume`, the row's inline field, `SidebarVolume.supportsRenaming`, `SidebarLocationMenu.normalizedVolumeName`, `FileBrowserState.renameVolume(at:from:to:)`/`volumeRenameMessage`, `AuthorizationPurpose.renameVolume`, the `volumeRenamer` dependency, `Zforge/Services/VolumeRenamer.swift`, and its 9 String Catalog keys. The volume branch in `commitRename()` was removed. New `FileBrowserState.canRename(_:)` excludes volume roots (`isVolume`) and gates `canRenameSelection` (File ▸ Rename and Return, the toolbar's More menu), the item context menu's Rename, and `beginRenaming(_:)`. `followRenamedVolume(from:to:)` and the `didRenameVolumeNotification` handling stay.
- **Tests changed for the new contract (not to make code pass):** `SidebarLocationMenuTests`: the Ventoy, boot and internal volume layouts end with Get Info; the Ventoy test asserts no Rename item and `SidebarVolumeMenuCommand.allCases == [.eject]`; `readOnlyVolumeOmitsRename` became `noVolumeOffersRename` (no Rename item or identifier for any volume kind); the localization and command-dispatch tests drop Rename; `volumeNames`, `renameBeginsInlineEditing`, `invalidVolumeNameIsRejected` and `contentRenameOfVolumeRootUsesVolumeRename` were removed with the behavior they covered; `followsRenamedVolume` stays. `ZforgeTests/VolumeRenameTests.swift` (suites “Volume rename” and “Volume rename on a disk image”) was deleted with the feature it covered.
- **New coverage (`ZforgeTests/VolumeRootRenameTests.swift`, suite “Volume roots can't be renamed”):** `startupVolumeCannotBeRenamed` (all four view modes): `/` at the computer level selected → `canRename`/`canRenameSelection` false, `beginRenamingSelectedItem` and `beginRenaming` start nothing, Rename disabled in the context menu and in the More menu; `folderCanStillBeRenamed`: a temporary folder keeps every entry point enabled and starts the inline rename, and a multiple selection disables it.
- **Results:** derived data `/tmp/zforge-dd-rename`; full `Unit` plan (log `/tmp/zforge-rename-test.log`, `/tmp/zforge-dd-rename/Logs/Test/Test-Zforge-2026.10.30_07-55-42--0300.xcresult`): **568 tests in 62 suites passed, 0 issues** (the known D-28, timing and `TagCatalogTests` failures did not occur in this run; `MDB_MAP_FULL` was still logged). `Scripts/affected-tests.py --check`: 26 UI tests, 21 rules, 0 problems (removed `VolumeRenamer.swift` from the map).
- **Not executed:** UI tests (they take over the screen). No UI test used volume rename. Mapped by this change's files (14): `AutomationUITests/testSettingsProvidesLocalizedAgentSetupAndNativeCancellation`, `LocalizationUITests/testLanguageSelectionUpdatesOpenMenuAndPersists`, and in `ZforgeUITests`: `testArchiveDragCopiesIntoSidebarFolder`, `testArchiveInternalCopyConfirmationKeepsHistoryAndAncestorColumns`, `testBlankAreaUsesMoreMenuInEveryView`, `testColumnPreviewRefitsAfterUserScrollsLeft`, `testCompactToolbarMenusOpenFromTheirToolbarButtons`, `testCustomCompressionPanelOpensFolderChooser`, `testFileOpenNavigatesSelectedFolderAndArchiveInColumnsMode`, `testKeyboardShortcutHelpOpensAndFilters`, `testListKeyboardZoomAndArrowNavigationAfterChangingView`, `testLocalDragsOptionCopyAndConfirmAdditionToArchive`, `testTagEditorIsSharedByToolbarAndContextMenu`, `testViewModeIsPerWindowAndLastClosedWindowRestoresIt`.
- **Pending:** in-app check of the Locations menu on a real drive and of Rename disabled for volume roots at the computer level (four modes); following a volume renamed in Finder or Disk Utility in the running app.

Three failures found by the offscreen XCUITest tool, each reproduced here in the real XCUITest mode on clean `7ea66ec` without any tool component. Derived data `/tmp/zf-fix/dd` (and the default one for the final runs); logs and result bundles under `/tmp/zf-fix/`.

- **Real baseline (before):** `base-search` fails `BrowserSearchUITests.swift:32` (`Welcome.txt` still listed after typing); `base-slider` fails `SettingsUITests.swift:100` (“1.03 GB” ≠ “1.06 GB”) and `:101`; `base-pair` passes `testBlankAreaUsesMoreMenuInEveryView` (72.3 s) then fails `ZforgeUITests.swift:914` “Missing Compress Selected”; `base-compact` (alone, run after the pair) fails at the same line. The compact failure's recording shows Gallery with `Welcome.txt` selected.
- **Diagnostic real runs (temporary logging, removed):** search field `@FocusState` read `false` after being set, after a yield and after 400 ms, with the first responder `KeyViewProxy`; AppKit `makeFirstResponder` on the toolbar's `AppKitTextField` focused it, but `@FocusState` stayed `false` and Escape did not collapse the field (failure moved to line 35). Slider: a slow `click(forDuration:thenDragTo:withVelocity:thenHoldForDuration:)` (10 pt at 20 pt/s) delivered 26 drag events whose AppKit proposals were the press position plus the pointer travel (absolute), and the value went 1 GB → 1.03 → 1.06 GB. `press(forDuration:thenDragTo:withVelocity:thenHoldForDuration:)` synthesized no events (“Could not find any displays containing rect (inf, inf, 0.0, 0.0)”); an earlier “stuck knob” reading based on it was withdrawn together with a slider code change (D-62).
- **Offscreen limits:** `NSSlider`'s tracking loop consumed neither queued events (`NSApp.postEvent` + `mouseDown`, also with the window ordered offscreen) nor events dispatched through `NSApp.sendEvent`/`NSWindow.sendEvent`; real slider tracking stays with the UI test.
- **New offscreen coverage:**
  - `BrowserWindowLayoutTests.findFocusesToolbarSearchField` (real `ContentView` window, 1,100 pt): content has focus at launch → Find → the window's first responder is the field editor of `toolbar-search-field`, `searchFieldHasFocus` → typing “Another” sets the query → Return lists only `Another.txt` and keeps focus → ⌘F again keeps focus → Escape restores the three Recents files, clears and collapses the field and gives the focus back to the same content responder. Before: failed at line 70 (no field focus). With field focus but without the focus return: failed at the final content-focus check. After: passes.
  - `BrowserWindowLayoutTests.emptySearchFieldCollapsesOnFocusLoss`: failed before (no field focus), passes after.
  - `UIFixturePreferencesIsolationTests` (3 tests, 4 executions): with an extraction keeping the old behavior, the fixture case failed for `--ui-fixtures` and `--ui-icon-preview-fixtures` (the launch used `.standard`, the next launch saw the previous launch's view mode); after, all pass. **Side effect:** that characterization run wrote `browser.viewMode = gallery` to the unit host's `.standard`, the user's own Zforge preferences (D-63).
  - `SettingsSliderTests.dragDistancesAtSettingsWidth`: characterization of the UI test's distances at 510 pt with AppKit's measured absolute proposals (passes on the unchanged slider code).
  - The field's snapshot references (`BrowserSearchPresentationTests.snapshots`, light/dark) pass unchanged with the AppKit field.
- **Test changed (justified in D-62):** `SettingsUITests.testArchiveSizeLimitSliderHoldsRoundValuesWhileDragging`: 2 pt hold check unchanged; the 8 pt jump becomes a slow 10 pt drag with the same “1.06 GB” value and row checks; a quick drag back must stop on “1 GB”. `BrowserSearchUITests` and `testCompactToolbarMenusOpenFromTheirToolbarButtons` are unchanged. `BrowserFixture` (offscreen) now injects `UIFixtureSearchService`, as `--ui-fixtures` does.
- **Unit plan, baseline `7ea66ec` in a clean worktree, screen locked** (`unit-base.xcresult`): 568 tests in 62 suites, 21 issues, 8 failing tests: seven `TagCatalogTests` (D-49 locked-session checkpoint cases plus “File events update tags…”), “A bounded 10000-child workload…” (timing) and `rapidColumnArchiveSelection…` (D-28). A first run with the changes, also locked, matched it (one more D-49 case, “Restored linked items revalidate…”).
- **Unit plan, final, through `Scripts/affected-tests.py --run`, screen unlocked:** 574 tests in 63 suites, **1 issue**: `TagCatalogTests` “File events update tags and results without navigating or refreshing”, preexisting (failing in the baseline; MDB_MAP_FULL environment, see the 2026-10-30 entries). The script stops before the UI tests when the Unit plan fails, so its UI command was run directly.
- **UI tests, final** (`final-ui.xcresult`, `xcodebuild test -testPlan UI-Full` with the 9 tests `Scripts/affected-tests.py` selects): **9 executed, 9 passed, 0 skipped**: `testCommandFFocusesToolbarSearchAndEscapeCollapsesIt` (14.3 s), `testArchiveSizeLimitSliderHoldsRoundValuesWhileDragging` (17.7 s), `testListColumnsReorderByDragging`, `testBlankAreaUsesMoreMenuInEveryView` (73.0 s) followed by `testCompactToolbarMenusOpenFromTheirToolbarButtons` (17.1 s, the order-dependent pair), `testFileOpenNavigatesSelectedFolderAndArchiveInColumnsMode`, `testListKeyboardZoomAndArrowNavigationAfterChangingView`, `testLocalDragsOptionCopyAndConfirmAdditionToArchive`, `testViewModeIsPerWindowAndLastClosedWindowRestoresIt`. ## Clearing the search from Recents — 2026-11-01 (D-64)

- **Report (user):** after searching from Recents, clearing the field left Recents empty until another action reloaded it.
- **Reproduction offscreen, failing for the expected reason:** `BrowserSearchStateTests.leavingSearchRestoresOriginListing` (5 origins × 3 exits; typing, deleting and Return go through the field's real scheduling with a controlled debounce) failed exactly 4 cases, Recents and tag × delete text and Return in the emptied field: the listing after leaving was empty. Folder, archive and Network origins and every Escape passed. The same test copied onto clean `7ea66ec` (worktree, `/tmp/zf-fix/clear-base.log`) fails the same 4 cases, so the defect predates `ToolbarSearchTextField` (D-61). `BrowserWindowLayoutTests.deletingSearchTextRestoresRecents` (real window, the AppKit field's editor deletes the text, four modes) failed in Icons, List, Columns and Gallery without the correction (line 116, empty listing).
- **The earlier contract test** `clearingAndEscape` called `applySearchText()` directly, outside the scheduling task, and checked the location but not the listing, so it could not see the defect. It is unchanged; the new tests add the missing paths.
- **After the correction:** both new tests pass (15 + 4 executions); the four search suites and the window suite pass (23 tests, `/tmp/zf-fix/clear-after.log`).
- **Impact map:** the “Shared state and models” rule now also selects `BrowserSearchUITests` (the search state lives in `FileBrowserState`); `--check`: 26 UI tests, 21 rules, 0 problems.
- **`Scripts/affected-tests.py --run`** (`/tmp/zf-fix/item1-run.log`): Unit plan **576 tests in 63 suites passed, 0 issues**. UI (6 selected): 5 passed (`testCommandFFocusesToolbarSearchAndEscapeCollapsesIt`, `testBlankAreaUsesMoreMenuInEveryView`, `testFileOpenNavigatesSelectedFolderAndArchiveInColumnsMode`, `testListKeyboardZoomAndArrowNavigationAfterChangingView`, `testViewModeIsPerWindowAndLastClosedWindowRestoresIt`); `testLocalDragsOptionCopyAndConfirmAdditionToArchive` failed at `ZforgeUITests.swift:562` (no “Add to Archive” dialog after the drag).
- **That drag failure, investigated (intermittent, cause not fully established):** the recording shows a Safari window covering the screen over the Zforge window during the drag, so the synthesized drag landed outside Zforge. A single rerun with the correction failed the same way with the Zforge window visible; its recording shows the pointer resting at the earlier File-menu click point for the whole “drag” (the drag never reached `Local.txt`). The same test then passed without the correction (`item1-drag-base`, 22.9 s) and with it (`item1-drag2`, 22.9 s); both failures lasted about 15 s. The D-64 change does not touch drag handling. Treated as environmental intermittency of window-server drag synthesis while the machine was in use; not counted as a pass, not hidden.

## Preferences audit — 2026-11-01 (ZF-54, D-63)

Read-only: a temporary unit test inside the test host (the only process with access to the `io.libor.Zforge` container) listed `UserDefaults.standard`'s persistent domain and counted the container's preference files; the probe was removed and nothing was written to the user's preferences.

- **`browser.viewMode` = `list`** (not `gallery`). It was overwritten after D-63's characterization run; fixed builds no longer write it from fixture launches, so the writer was most likely a real-mode fixture run of a build without the fix (for example the offscreen tool's comparison builds from `7ea66ec` sources). The user's original value is unknown.
- **Values a pre-fix fixture launch could have written** (FileBrowserPreferences in `.standard`), with the values found: `browser.scale` 1, `browser.listColumns` [name, modificationDate, size, kind, tags], `browser.listColumnWidths` (modificationDate, name, size), `browser.directoryListColumns` (`/`, `~/Desktop`), `browser.finderColumnWidth` 458.23, `browser.previewWidth` 300.73, `browser.previewVisible` icons 0 / list 1 / columns 1 / gallery 0 (the default is 1), `browser.sortField` name, `browser.sortAscending` 0, `browser.showHidden` 0, `browser.knownSidebarTags` (25 entries, including test names such as “Zforge live <UUID> changed”, “Zforge live <UUID>”, “Zforge navigation <UUID>”, “Zforge Efficiency Validation”), `appLanguagePreference` system, `archive.nestingLimit` 5, `archive.rootSizeLimitBytes` 2,000,000,000, `archive.defaultCompressionFormat` 7z, two compression profiles (default: the second). Which of these the user chose cannot be told from the data.
- **AppKit and other state written by any launch, fixtures included:** `NSWindow Frame browser-AppWindow-1` (720 × 720) and `-2` (1,100 × 720), the fixture window widths, plus frames for the Settings, Keyboard Shortcuts and Go To windows and `Zforge.ContentView-1-AppWindow-1`; `NSSplitView Subview Frames` for five browser windows; open-panel state (`NSOSPLastRootDirectory`, `NSNavPanelExpandedSizeForOpenMode`, and six `automation-contextual-access - …` keys from `AutomationPermissionUIFixture`'s panel); `automationSecurityScopedFolders` (`~/Documents`, `~/Music`, `/tmp/ZforgeAutomationSmoke-20261007`, the last a test artefact); `securityScopedBookmarks` (`/`, `~`; fixture launches use `UIFixtureAccessGrantStore`, so these come from ordinary launches); `sidebarPinnedFolders.v1` (the four defaults: Desktop, Documents, Downloads, Applications).
- **Container clutter:** 35,561 preference files (1.6 MB), of which 35,555 are leftover unit and UI test suites named `<Name>-<UUID>` (most numerous: ArchiveNavigation 5,747, ZforgeLocalization 1,606, ArchiveOutbound 1,565, SiriArchiveTests 1,401); `removePersistentDomain` empties a suite but leaves its file. `ZforgeUIFixturePreferences.plist` exists and is empty, as D-63 intends.
- **What the user could restore (not done):** Default View (`browser.viewMode`); the test tag names in `browser.knownSidebarTags`; the `/tmp/ZforgeAutomationSmoke-20261007` automation folder; optionally the fixture window frames and split-view frames (720/1,100 pt windows) and the preview visibility in Icons and Gallery if not chosen; deleting the leftover `<Name>-<UUID>` suite files.

## Search field visual review and narrow widths — 2026-11-01 (D-66)

- **Method:** temporary unit tests rendered the real `ContentView` window offscreen, toolbar included (`cacheDisplay` of the window frame view), in three states — closed, open (empty, focused), typed “Another” with results — and read the field's accessibility attributes. Images were reviewed and not committed: offscreen captures draw no sidebar material and no file thumbnails, and caught one glass transition mid-way, so they are not stable snapshot references. The field itself keeps its committed references (`BrowserSearchPresentationTests.snapshots`, light/dark).
- **Seen, current code:** at 1,100 pt in Icons, List, Columns and Gallery (light), List dark and List German: closed shows the magnifying-glass button; open shows the 200 pt field with its magnifying glass and the placeholder (“Search”, “Suchen”); typed shows the text, the clear button and the title “Searching “Another”” (“Suche nach „Another“”, German quotation marks). The new field's placeholder uses the system placeholder color, lighter than the old SwiftUI field's darker placeholder; text, alignment and clear button match. Accessibility: label “Search” (“Suchen”), identifier `toolbar-search-field`, the window's first responder is the field's editor after Find; the real-window UI test now also asserts the accessibility keyboard focus (`hasKeyboardFocus`) and the label, which is what VoiceOver follows. Reduce Motion: `BrowserToolbarSearchControl.expansionAnimation(reduceMotion:)` returns no animation with Reduce Motion and `snappy(0.22)` otherwise (`BrowserSearchPresentationTests.expansionAnimationHonorsReduceMotion`). No real VoiceOver session was run (it would take over the user's system).

A temporary real-window UI test (removed) confirmed it at 720 and 1,000 pt: no field after ⌘F, typing filtered nothing.
- **Correction verified:** all 29 widths pass; `ToolbarLayoutTests.expandedSearchStages` pins the search-aware breakpoints (1,050 / 990 / 828 pt) and the hidden title below 828 pt. Captures after the correction: 640 and 720 pt open — title removed, double-chevron menu and the full field; 1,000 pt open — compact view selector, archive action, menu and the full field, title kept; German dark 720 pt correct. Experiments rejected: flexible field width (`NSToolbar` ignored it); a narrower field in the smallest stage (35 pt of text at 640 pt).
- **Unit plan with the correction, screen locked** (`/tmp/zf-fix/item3-unit.log`): 581 tests, 20 issues, all in the known locked-session set (D-49 `TagCatalogTests`, the 10,000-child timing case, D-28). UI tests (screen unlocked, no lock during the run, 11:12–11:21; `/tmp/zf-fix/item3-ui.xcresult`; the 19 tests `Scripts/affected-tests.py` selects for this tree, which also holds the ZF-54 isolation): **19 executed, 19 passed** — `AutomationUITests` ×2, `BrowserSearchUITests` (with the new keyboard-focus and label assertions), `IconPreviewUITests`, `LocalizationUITests`, `SettingsUITests` ×2, and in `ZforgeUITests`: blank-area More menu, compact toolbar menus, custom compression folder chooser, File ▸ Open (Columns and List), folder selection menu, shortcut help, List zoom/arrows, local drags (D-65 passed this time), New Folder with Selection, tag editor, per-window view mode. ## Unit test host isolation — 2026-11-01 (D-68, ZF-54)

- **Characterization, failing before** (`/tmp/zf-fix/host-before.log`): `UnitTestHostIsolationTests` — resolver returned `.standard` for a test-host environment; `ZforgeDefaults.application === .standard` in the host; no window autosave isolation in the host; `removePersistentDomain` left the suite's file. All four failed.
- **Findings during the work:** (1) the host's access to files outside its container came from the user's `/` and `~` grants, read from the user's preferences: once isolated, writes into the project were denied (snapshot recording, probes); reading still works. (2) Removing a suite and deleting its file in the run does not last: cfprefsd writes the cached domain back when the host exits (files appeared with the run's end time, empty after `removePersistentDomain`, with values after a plain delete). (3) A first version of the host test called the resolver with a host environment and cleared the live host suite, failing `AutomationTests.nativeActions` (`authorizationRequired`) in the full run; replaced by the pure `ZforgeDefaults.storage`.
- **After:** `UnitTestHostIsolationTests` (5), `UIFixturePreferencesIsolationTests` (5), `UITestLaunchIsolationTests` (4, stand-in window and split view, bounded split-view search) pass. Snapshot recording through an attachment verified: a missing reference is attached as `ZforgeSnapshots__<test file>__<name>.png`, `Scripts/export-snapshots.py` copied it into the project, and the next comparison passed (probe removed).
- **Preference re-audit** (temporary probes attach the real domain and the container's file list; diff outside the sandbox): around a full `Unit` run (592 tests) and a following short run, the user's domain had **no key added, removed or changed**; the full run left 361 `ZforgeTestSuite.` files and the short run's sweep deleted all of them (17 of its own remained), plus 5 unprefixed `IconPreviewGrid-` files, since prefixed. Before that, 1,387 suite files from this session's runs were removed with a backup (`~/Library/Caches/zforge-prefs-backup-2026-11-01-followup/session-suites/`); `ZforgeProbe-` ×3 and `dump-` ×1 (older, unknown origin) were kept.
- **Full `Unit` run failures:** “File events update tags…” (known intermittent), `rapidColumnArchiveSelection…` (D-28), and the first sweep test, which swept the shared preferences folder in parallel with other tests — it now runs on a folder of its own and passes.

- **After all changes:** `fix2` (sampled, load mean 53.3) and validation runs `val1` (16 processes, load mean 72.4, max 105.3), `val2` (16, mean 100.7, max 110.0), `val3` (24, mean 108.9, max 126.7): **597 tests each, no `IconPreviewServiceTests` time limit, no rapid column timeout, no host restart.** Remaining in each: `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests:52` (file-reading time over 250 ms; preexisting timing bound, also at `c3c6730`) and `TagCatalogTests:1626` (D-49, preexisting). ## Quick tag row: ten circles at the sidebar dot size — 2026-11-01

User request: the context/overflow menu circles were too large; show up to ten and draw them at the sidebar tag dot size.

- Implementation: `FinderTagPresentation.quickTagLimit` = 10; `FinderTagQuickButton` draws a 12 pt circle (`SidebarTagDot.size`, shared with the sidebar) centered in an 18 pt hit area, 22 pt pitch, 28 pt row; hover/applied glyphs are 7 pt heavy, fitted without distortion. Fixed in the same change: an uncolored tag (index 0) was filled opaque black because `NSColor.clear.withAlphaComponent(1)` is black; it is now an outline only, as in the sidebar.
- New/changed unit coverage: `choosesFolderColor` caps 14 tags at 10 (was 10 → 7, expectation changed by the user's request); new `quickTagRowGeometry` (ten of twelve catalog tags, circle diameter equals `SidebarTagDot.size` = 12, every hit area inside the row, non-overlapping, vertically centered); new snapshot `quickTagRowSnapshot` light/dark (`ZforgeSnapshots/TagCatalogTests/`), recorded on the first run and viewed before commit: ten 12 pt circles, + on the hovered first circle, check on the applied Red, outline-only uncolored tag. Test titles/comments that said “seven” as the cap now refer to the seven fixture tags. `TagCatalogTests` + `BrowserMenuParityTests` against the recorded references: **71 passed**, zero failures (`/tmp/zf-final.log`).
- Full Unit plan with the change (`affected-tests.py --run`, `Test-Zforge-2026.11.01_20-56-06--0300.xcresult`): 599 tests, 4 issues in 2 tests, both outside this change: `TagCatalogTests.updatesGlobalCatalogueLive` (FSEvents wait, 3 issues) and `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` (250 ms budget). Unchanged `main` baseline (changes stashed, `/tmp/zf-baseline.log`): 597 tests, 8 issues, also failing `updatesGlobalCatalogueLive`, plus `rapidColumnArchiveSelection…`, a device-relative monitoring test and `Persistent replay … hidden directory`. **Preexisting/intermittent under load**, not introduced here.
- Mapped UI tests (7, run directly because the script stops on the Unit failures; `/tmp/zf-ui.log`): 5 passed; `testArchiveDragCopiesIntoSidebarFolder` failed on an XCUITest screenshot timeout (unrelated, infrastructure); `testTagEditorIsSharedByToolbarAndContextMenu` failed once at line 717 (editor title stayed `Red` after moving from the Red circle onto Edit Tags: the row's `mouseExited` was not delivered), then passed 2/2 alone (`/tmp/zf-tagui-1.xcresult`, `/tmp/zf-tagui-2.xcresult`). **Intermittent, open:** the row relies on `mouseExited` to restore the editor title; a deterministic fix (clear on `menu(_:willHighlight:)` for another item) needs delegate chaining with `TrackedToolbarMenu` and is proposed, not done.
- Pending: visual check of the real menu in the four modes, Portuguese and zoom; the snapshot covers light/dark English at 100 %.

## In-place archive reading — ZF-55 stage 1, 2026-11-02 (D-73)

Contract: OPEN-REQUIREMENTS “In-place archive reading, nested-archive removal and preview limits (D-73)”, stage 1 (engine). Performance acceptance is structural, not timed (user, 2026-11-02): no test measures time against archive size.

- **Baseline** (before any change, `HEAD` `df1ff6e`, clean build, `/tmp/zf55/baseline.xcresult`): `Unit` plan **597 tests, 596 passed, 1 failed**: `TagCatalogTests:1626` (D-49, preexisting). Build: 0 warnings.
- **Characterization of the affected contracts before the change:** opening, browsing and nested listing (`NativeSevenZipTests`, `ZforgeTests` format round trips, `ArchiveMaterializationConcurrencyTests`), materialization and limits (`NativeSevenZipTests.outputLimit`, `advancedProperties`, `splitVolumes`), extraction and conflicts (`partialSelectionMapping`, `selectedExtractionConflicts`, symlink cases), validation (`corruptedItem`, `testArchive` in the format tests), add/remove/reorganize and change detection (`ArchiveMutationTests`: `deletion`, `internalTransfers`, `internalSafety`, `concurrentReorganization`, `cancelledReorganization`, outbound transfers and promises), drag-out (`ArchiveDragDropTests`), automation opening scope (`SiriArchiveIntentTests`). Gaps found and covered by the new suite: absence of archive copies, bounded source reads, fingerprint, compressed tar listing/extraction (no test existed), export without staging, parent index equivalence, solid-block metadata.
- **New suite `ArchiveInPlaceReadingTests`** (17 tests, 25 cases with arguments; all pass, `/tmp/zf55/run3.xcresult` and the final run): opening ZIP/7z creates no session folder and no copy (file-manager spy plus a content-hash scan of Zforge's temporary storage); split 7z/ZIP volumes are read in place and the session holds only the materialized item; listing reads are bounded by the index (instrumented source stream: below 1 MiB and below 1/16 of a 16 MiB archive; browsing adds no read); materialization stores only the item; extraction and validation create no copy and no working folder; add/remove (ZIP, 7z) write once beside the original — no copy, no working folder, no leftovers, the original replaced, folders and links added from their paths; fingerprint unit test (date, same-size-and-date replacement, size, removal); a changed archive makes materialization, item extraction and export fail with no output and removal report the archive-changed error with the original untouched; export of files and folders (ordinary and promised) lands directly at the destination, never replaces an existing promise destination; parent index equals the previous full scan for Unicode, canonically equivalent, combining-mark and empty-component paths; folder listings through the index; compressed tar (`.tar.gz`, `.tar.bz2`, `.tar.xz` created by the engine, `.tar.zst` fixture `Fixtures/stream.tar.zst`) lists, materializes, extracts items and extracts fully with no temporary tar and no working folder; the first entry of a 16 MiB `.tar.gz` is materialized reading less than half of the archive (early stop); a truncated `.tar.gz` fails to open; solid 7z members share one block whose size is below the archive's, non-solid 7z, ZIP and directories have none; compressed tar and single-file `.gz`/`.xz`/`.bz2` use the whole archive size.
- **The new structural tests fail on the previous implementation** (temporary worktree at `df1ff6e` with the subset that compiles against the old API, `/tmp/zf55/old.xcresult`): **9 tests, 7 failed, 2 passed** — failures for the expected reasons: copies through the file manager (opening, materialization, extraction, add, export, split volumes), session folders created at opening, the `StreamPayload` tar in the session, `Operations` working folders; the 2 characterization tests (truncated `.tar.gz`, folder listing) pass on both.
- **Measurements (instrumented source stream, Debug, one run):** listing a 16,809,562-byte stored ZIP (one 16 MiB file and 200 small files) reads 282,235 bytes; a 16,781,652-byte 7z reads 1,746 bytes; a 16,787,920-byte `.tar.gz` lists in one pass reading 17,836,504 bytes (the gzip handler's open probe adds about 1 MB); materializing its first entry read 6,291,464 bytes with the initial 4 MiB pipe, after which the pipe was reduced to 1 MiB.
- **Existing tests changed to the new contract (D-73, user decision of 2026-11-01/02):** `SiriArchiveIntentTests.openingScopeLifetime` (`success`) and `encryptedOpeningScope` (`complete`) expected the automation grant to be released right after a successful opening; with in-place reading the window keeps it while the archive is shown, so both now assert it is kept and released when the window closes. Error, cancellation, dismissal and close cases are unchanged. No other existing test changed its expectations.
- **Warnings:** the new suite's `FileManager` subclass made the compiler report the existing `FolderWithSelectionTests` file managers' `@unchecked Sendable` (an unavailable conformance; it was not reported in the baseline build). The two classes need no `Sendable` (they are handed to an actor like the engine's spy), so the redundant conformance was removed; scenarios and assertions unchanged. Build: 0 warnings.
- **Final `Scripts/affected-tests.py --run --no-ui`** (2026-11-02 01:14–01:16, `/tmp/zf55/final-unit.log`, after all changes): `Unit` plan **614 tests (597 + 17 new), 612 passed, 2 failed, 0 skipped**, both preexisting: `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` (file-reading time over 250 ms under load; **passes alone**, `/tmp/zf55/disc.log`) and `TagCatalogTests:1626` (D-49). `--no-ui` because the script would run the real-only UI group after a passing Unit plan; the UI selection ran offscreen as below. `Scripts/affected-tests.py --check`: 26 UI tests, 22 rules, 0 problems (no new source files).
- **UI tests selected for the change (14):** 7 simulable ran **offscreen** through `zforge-xcuitest-sim` (screen guard: 0 front changes, 0 visible windows, 0 synthetic events) in a fresh build folder: **7 executed, 7 passed** (`affected-20261102-011158*`): `testBlankAreaUsesMoreMenuInEveryView`, `testLanguageSelectionUpdatesOpenMenuAndPersists`, `testListKeyboardZoomAndArrowNavigationAfterChangingView`, `testKeyboardShortcutHelpOpensAndFilters`, `testFileOpenNavigatesSelectedFolderAndArchiveInColumnsMode`, `testViewModeIsPerWindowAndLastClosedWindowRestoresIt`, `testCommandFFocusesToolbarSearchAndEscapeCollapsesIt`. A first offscreen run in the shared `zforge-affected-tests` build folder failed `testFileOpenNavigatesSelectedFolderAndArchiveInColumnsMode` twice at `ZforgeUITests.swift:282` (the title captured when the window appeared was still “Recents”) while Finder kept the Finder extension of that build copy running (D-67), which also blocked rebuilding there; in fresh build folders the test **passed** both at `df1ff6e` and with this change, and in the final run above. The test reads the window title as soon as the window exists, so it depends on the fixture being created and opened by then.
- **Skipped (real-only, they take over the screen; not run, per the request):** `AutomationUITests.testSettingsProvidesLocalizedAgentSetupAndNativeCancellation`, `FinderExtensionUITests.testFinderSubmenuOffersArchiveActionsAndExtractsInZforge`, `ZforgeUITests.testArchiveCopyIntoAnotherZforgeWindow`, `testArchiveDragCopiesIntoSidebarFolder`, `testArchiveInternalCopyConfirmationKeepsHistoryAndAncestorColumns`, `testArchivePromisesPublishIntoFinderBeforeRemovingOriginal`, `testLocalDragsOptionCopyAndConfirmAdditionToArchive`. In particular the real Finder promise drop (sandboxed write to the exact promised URL) is covered only offscreen by `ArchiveMutationTests` promise cases and `ArchiveInPlaceReadingTests.exportPublishesDirectly`, not in the real sandbox with Finder.
- **Not covered / limits:** RAR, CAB and WIM/ESD solid-block metadata (no fixtures; RAR cannot be created here); sparse tar entries in a compressed tar (no fixture; rejected by design); cancellation of an in-place read by closing its session (no deterministic test; the read is registered under the session folder that `closeSession` cancels); reading from removable and SMB volumes, ejection and disconnection (not exercised); visual review not applicable (no UI change). `createArchive` still stages its sources and copies its output (outside stage 1).

## Inner archive windows and shared sessions — ZF-55 stage 2, 2026-11-02 (D-73)

Contract: OPEN-REQUIREMENTS D-73 bullets “Remove nested archives”, “Archive inside an archive”, “Shared sessions for every archive” and “Write-back rules”. Structural tests only (no timed tests).

- **Baseline** (stage 1 commit `4e60cb7`, before any stage-2 change): the stage-1 final run (614 tests, 2 preexisting failures) is the reference; the first stage-2 run after the nesting removal (`/tmp/zf55/s2r1.xcresult`) showed only the expected contract failures listed below plus the two preexisting ones.
- **Existing tests changed to the new contract (user decisions of 2026-11-01, D-73):** `ZforgeTests` inline/navigation predicates (an archive inside an archive is neither navigable nor expandable and `opensAsInnerArchive`), `nestedArchivePolicy` → `archiveAddressAppending`, `archivePreferences` (removed keys deleted), `listInlineArchiveExpansion` (inner archive not expandable), `nestedArchive` → `innerArchiveSession`, `columnNestedArchiveExtractionPreservesHierarchy` (same three-level archive and the same expected extraction path, now through inner windows); `blockedNestedArchive` and `legacyArchiveRootSizeLimit` removed with the policy and preference they tested; `SettingsSliderTests.settingsSection` → `settingsWithoutNavigationLimits` (snapshot references `archive-navigation-{light,dark,dark-pt-BR}` deleted); `SettingsUITests.testArchiveSizeLimitSliderHoldsRoundValuesWhileDragging` drags the same slider and scale through the `--ui-scale-slider-fixture` Settings fixture (assertions unchanged); `ArchiveMutationTests.mutationPreservesNavigation` and `ZforgeTests.archiveAdditionAfterDialogDismissal` assert the same session identifier after a write (shared sessions updated in place) instead of a new one; `ArchiveMutationTests` stale-session cases (`Removal refuses a stale session…`, `internalSafety`) make the outside change with another engine instance, because the engine's own writes now update its sessions (scenario and assertions unchanged); `ArchiveDragDropTests` read drop addresses through `archiveDropAddress(for:)` (same semantics). Test fakes adopt the new protocol (no policy, no nested opening).
- **New suite `InnerArchiveWindowTests`** (17 tests, 19 cases; 4 runs of the suite, all passing): shared root session and reference counting; joining an archive with encrypted names needs its password; a change through one client updates the shared session in place (same identifiers, revision 1, notification) and each client releases only its references; an inner archive's writable copy lives in its own session folder, keeps the outer session open after the outer window closes, and is removed with its session; a modified inner archive is written back on the last release (entry replaced, outer relisted and announced, no leftovers); chains write back innermost first through the coordinator (progress order `C.zip → A.zip › B.zip`, then `B.zip → A.zip`); outer changed elsewhere → Replace Anyway (both changes kept), Save Inner Archive As (saved copy, outer untouched), Discard (outer untouched); a failed write-back (folder refusing the hidden file) keeps the copy and Try Again succeeds; a cancelled write-back keeps the copy for another attempt; an encrypted outer archive uses the session password and asks only when none is known; an inner archive of a compressed tar is read-only (engine refuses removal and addition); state level: Open on an inner archive requests a new window that adopts the session (title and path `Outer.zip › Inner.zip`, reference moved, second Open joins the same session); a deletion in one window reaches another window of the archive keeping its location and selection; a folder removed elsewhere moves the other window to the nearest surviving folder; closing the last window of an archive closes its inner windows innermost first (`C`, then `B`) and not while another window still shows it; closing a modified inner window writes it back and the outer window reloads keeping its selection; a read-only inner window offers no remove, reorganize, add or drop.
- **Bug found and fixed during the runs:** one Unit run crashed the test host (01:51, `EXC_BAD_ACCESS` in 7-Zip's `NZip::CHandler::Extract`, `~/Library/Logs/DiagnosticReports/Zforge-2026-11-02-015115.ips`): extraction lists the archive and then reopens it; read in place, the archive can be replaced in between by another writer, and an index from the first listing past the new archive's items was passed to the handler, which reads out of bounds. The bridge now refuses such an index. Regression test `ArchiveInPlaceReadingTests.extractionRejectsStaleIndex` crashes the host without the guard (verified: “Restarting after unexpected exit, crash”) and passes with it. Residual risk, not covered: if the replaced archive has at least as many items, an index can name another item; the engine's fingerprint checks before and after each read report the change, and previews and exports discard their output, but item extraction to a user folder may leave what was written. Two crash dialogs these runs left on the screen (“Zforge quit unexpectedly”) blocked the offscreen tool and were dismissed with Ignore; nothing else on the screen was touched.
- **Final `Scripts/affected-tests.py --run --no-ui`** (02:16, `/tmp/zf55/s2-final-unit.log`): `Unit` plan **630 tests, 629 passed, 1 failed, 0 skipped**: `TagCatalogTests:1626` (D-49, preexisting). `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests` passed in this run (it failed its 250 ms bound in other runs under load, preexisting). Build: 0 source warnings. `--check`: 27 UI tests, 22 rules, 0 problems (`testDoubleClickInnerArchiveOpensItInNewWindow` mapped to the archive engine and window lifecycle rules; `ArchiveSessionClient.swift` mapped).
- **UI tests selected (27): 17 offscreen, all passed** (fresh build folder, `affected-20261102-021811*`, screen guard 0 front changes, 0 visible windows, 0 synthetic events), including the new `testDoubleClickInnerArchiveOpensItInNewWindow` (double click opens `Sample.zip › Inner.zip` in a second window with its content, the outer window stays) and the slider drag on the fixture. Earlier offscreen attempt: the new test failed because the UI-fixture initializer ignored the session handoff (fixed); `testCompactToolbarMenusOpenFromTheirToolbarButtons` timed out once (2 s) in the parallel run and passed alone and in the final run. **10 real-only tests not run** (they take over the screen): `AutomationUITests.testMCPContextualPermissionCancellationAndDisconnect`, `testSettingsProvidesLocalizedAgentSetupAndNativeCancellation`, `FinderExtensionUITests.testFinderSubmenuOffersArchiveActionsAndExtractsInZforge`, `ZforgeUITests.testArchiveCopyIntoAnotherZforgeWindow`, `testArchiveDragCopiesIntoSidebarFolder`, `testArchiveInternalCopyConfirmationKeepsHistoryAndAncestorColumns`, `testArchivePromisesPublishIntoFinderBeforeRemovingOriginal`, `testCustomCompressionPanelOpensFolderChooser`, `testListHeaderDividerResizesAndHeaderBodyReorders`, `testLocalDragsOptionCopyAndConfirmAdditionToArchive`.
- **Not covered / limits:** the quit path (`applicationShouldTerminate` closing windows and replying after the write-backs) has no automated test (the registry's close order and `finishPendingWriteBacks` are tested separately); the write-back panel and alerts (`ArchiveWriteBackPanelPresenter`) have no snapshot or visual review and the real alert/save-panel flow was not exercised; window restoration of inner windows is not attempted by design; localization of the 12 new strings checked by the catalog audit only (no review by native speakers).

## Preview limits and opening progress — ZF-55 stage 3, 2026-11-02 (D-73)

Contract: OPEN-REQUIREMENTS D-73 (“Preview size limit”, “Thumbnail size limit”, “Solid archives preview checkbox”, “Removable and remote volumes”, “Sliders”, “Solid blocks”, “Thumbnails in solid blocks”, the review decisions and “Accepted additions”), plus item 0 (the stage-2 race). Structural tests only; no timing against archive size.

- **Item 0 — extraction never trusts an earlier listing:** the bridge lists and extracts from one opening, after the engine confirms the opened files still match the session's fingerprint, before any folder or file is written. New tests in `ArchiveInPlaceReadingTests`: `replacedArchiveIsNeverExtracted` (ZIP and 7z replaced with the same paths and more items between listing and extraction: “archive changed”, nothing written) and `replacementAfterOpeningIsDetected` (the replacement happens after the read opened the archive, through the check itself: nothing written). The stage-2 `extractionRejectsStaleIndex` guard stays.
- **New suite `ArchivePreviewLimitTests`** (15 tests, 23 cases; run 3 times alone, then in the full plan): volume classification; measure (item, solid block, unknown) and the preview/thumbnail decisions on internal, removable and network volumes, with the solid setting and min(thumbnail, volume) thumbnails; preferences defaults, clamping and persistence; the preview (32 MB–2 GB) and thumbnail (4–64 MB) slider presets (equal doublings, 1/32 steps, held round values, dragging reaches every step) and their decimal tick labels; browser state: a single selection under the preview limit (9 MB loads), a multiple selection under the thumbnail limit (9 MB waits for the manual load), removable/network archives preview nothing automatically and read nothing until enabled, the manual load still works, solid items follow Preview solid archives, grid thumbnails follow the thumbnail limit and measure; opening progress hidden before the delay and shown after it with “Opening “Slow.tar.gz”…”, Cancel opening nothing with no error; cancelling a whole-stream opening reaches the bridge (`cancelledOperations == 1`) and leaves no session; a fast selection change cancels the previous preview's native read in the bridge (a read held in flight through the source-read observer). The legacy 256 MB–16 GB scale and all `SettingsSliderTests` are unchanged and pass.
- **Snapshots (9 references, all reviewed before committing):** Settings ▸ Previews in Archives in English and German, light and dark (all switches on to show every slider); the write-back progress panel (light, dark); the write-back conflict, failure and password alerts. A first recording caught the network slider knob mid-update and the panel without layout; both tests were corrected and re-recorded.
- **Existing test changed to the new contract:** `ZforgeTests` “Rapid column archive selection cancels the previous load…” expected the opening text “Loading…”; the requested opening progress names the archive: “Opening “First.zip”…”. `IconPreviewService.archiveByteLimit` is now the default thumbnail limit (8 MB); `IconPreviewServiceTests.automaticArchiveLimits` is unchanged and passes.
- **Bug found while running the new suite:** the new preference setters recursed (a property passed `inout` from its own `didSet` re-triggered it) and crashed the isolated run's host (stack overflow, `Zforge-2026-11-02-023713.ips`); fixed before any other run. The crash dialog it left was dismissed with Ignore.
- **Final `Scripts/affected-tests.py --run --no-ui`** (`/tmp/zf55/s3-final-unit.log`): `Unit` plan **647 tests, 646 passed, 1 failed, 0 skipped**: `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` (250 ms timing bound under load, preexisting; **passes alone**). `TagCatalogTests:1626` (D-49) passed in this run. Build: 0 source warnings. `--check`: 27 UI tests, 22 rules, 0 problems (`ArchivePreviewPolicy.swift` mapped).
- **UI tests selected (27): 17 offscreen** (fresh build folder, `affected-20261102-024510*`, screen guard 0 front changes, 0 visible windows, 0 synthetic events): **16 passed, 1 failed** — `testFileOpenNavigatesSelectedFolderAndArchiveInColumnsMode` at line 282, again the title read before the fixture folder had opened (also seen in stage 2). Alone before the fix it failed 1 of 2. **Test synchronization corrected** (the title is read after the existing wait for the fixture's rows; scenario and assertions unchanged); after it, **2 of 2 passed**; a third attempt and the final full offscreen rerun could not start because the console session became locked (“the console session is locked”). **10 real-only tests not run:** `AutomationUITests.testMCPContextualPermissionCancellationAndDisconnect`, `testSettingsProvidesLocalizedAgentSetupAndNativeCancellation`, `FinderExtensionUITests.testFinderSubmenuOffersArchiveActionsAndExtractsInZforge`, `ZforgeUITests.testArchiveCopyIntoAnotherZforgeWindow`, `testArchiveDragCopiesIntoSidebarFolder`, `testArchiveInternalCopyConfirmationKeepsHistoryAndAncestorColumns`, `testArchivePromisesPublishIntoFinderBeforeRemovingOriginal`, `testCustomCompressionPanelOpensFolderChooser`, `testListHeaderDividerResizesAndHeaderBodyReorders`, `testLocalDragsOptionCopyAndConfirmAdditionToArchive`.
- **Not covered / limits:** real removable and SMB/AFP/NFS volumes (classification uses injected kinds in tests; `ArchiveVolumeKind.of` only checked on the internal temporary folder); the real Settings window, the progress overlay timing and the write-back panel/alerts in real windows (offscreen snapshots only); Quick Look's progress path (it presents the system panel, not driven in tests); the 12 new strings checked by the catalog audit only.

## Warning commit gate — ZF-57, 2026-11-02 (RNF-Q07)

The user reported two Xcode warnings (`ComputerIdentity.swift` `String(cString:)`, `IconPreviewView.swift` closure in `@Entry`) and asked for commits with warnings to be blocked. Both were already fixed in `274ebf4`. **Clean build** of `c1c5d47` (fresh derived data, `build-for-testing`, scheme `Zforge`, Debug; `/tmp/zf-warncheck.log`): 0 warnings, 0 errors, so the reported warnings came from an older build still shown in Xcode.

- **Gate, first build** (`Scripts/check-warnings.py`, private copy in the worktree's git directory): 162 s, 0 warnings.
- **Blocking, through `git commit` with `core.hooksPath=.githooks`:** reintroducing `String(cString:)` in `ComputerIdentity.swift` and staging it: commit blocked, `ComputerIdentity.swift:51:9` deprecation reported (36 s, incremental). Then staging only an unrelated change in `IconPreviewView.swift` with the warning file unchanged: still blocked with the same warning (28 s), so the cached build did not hide it. With the warning only in the working tree (unstaged): gate passes (22 s), because it checks the index. All test edits were reverted; no test commit was created.
- **Script tests:** `python3 -m unittest discover -s Scripts/tests`: 33 passed (13 new for the gate: documentation-only skip, diagnostics parsing, third-party packages, locationless warnings, errors, index mirror with modes, symlinks, removal and preserved dates, invalidation).
- **`Scripts/affected-tests.py --run`** (`/tmp/zf57/affected.log`): selection 0 UI tests (`.githooks/*` added to the impact map's `ignore`; `--check` 27 UI tests, 22 rules, 0 problems). `Unit` plan: 652 tests, **2 failed**, both recorded load-sensitive failures that this change (scripts, hooks and documents only) cannot affect: `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests` (`fileTime < 250 ms`, `:52`) and `TagCatalogTests` “File events update tags…” (`:1688`, FSEvents wait).
- **Limits:** the gate builds Debug only; Release-only diagnostics are not checked. Worktrees whose branch predates `.githooks/` have no gate until they include it.

## Settings polish — ZF-62, 2026-11-09 (D-80)

Contract: CURRENT-STATE “Settings window in two columns … and its polish (ZF-62)”; UI-UX-GUIDE “Settings window in two columns — ZF-58 … polished by ZF-62”. Branch `t3/17987353` (base `6be2991`). Ids ZF-62 and D-80 were chosen high on purpose (another agent redesigns Keyboard Shortcuts in parallel).

- **Baseline before the change:** the untouched `6be2991` unit plan in a separate checkout: 471 Swift Testing cases passed; failing at the baseline already: `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` (known, see “Localization baseline findings”), the snapshot suites “each pane” (2 issues), “Settings: Previews in Archives” (2), “a profile's page” (1) and “Write-back alerts” (1) in that fresh checkout, and the test host crashes once per run (`EXC_BAD_ACCESS` in `objc_release` under `AutoreleasePoolPage::releaseUntil`, then “Restarting after unexpected exit”), which loses a few hundred XCTest cases from the “Failing tests” list. The crash is the same with this change (reports `Zforge-2026-11-09-203608.ips`, `-203229.ips`); not investigated here, not introduced by it.
- **New and changed tests (`SettingsWindowTests`):** flat sidebar in the old order; tile colours are the six system colours and lighter only at the top; every pane title in every one of the 20 languages fits two lines in 140 pt (title wrap, no truncation); the sidebar table has six rows of 28 pt with no gap; **title bar measured in the offscreen window, hosted as the browser window is**: 52 pt, 15 pt `SFNS-Semibold`, `labelColor`, leading-aligned with the 20 pt margin at the 220 pt sidebar edge, traffic lights centred (the same probe on the offscreen browser window gave 52 pt / 15 pt semibold / `labelColor`; before the change the Settings window had 32 pt and a 13 pt headline); the slider keeps its 484 pt track. Changed expectations are listed in D-80 (they encoded the removed grouping, the old gradient rule and the grouped form reference).
- **Snapshots (viewed before accepting; recorded with `TEST_RUNNER_ZFORGE_RECORD_SNAPSHOTS=1`, exported with `Scripts/export-snapshots.py`):** `ZforgeSnapshots/SettingsWindowTests/` — View Options (light, dark German, light Spanish), Sidebar pane with a fixed computer and home name (light, dark, light Spanish), Compression (light, dark, light Portuguese), Previews in Archives (light, dark), sidebar rows with the six tiles (light, dark, light Spanish, dark German); `ZforgeSnapshots/CompressionProfileSettingsTests/` (profile list light and dark, profile page ×3) and `ZforgeSnapshots/ArchivePreviewLimitTests/` (Previews ×4) re-recorded for the new layout (the latter two tests now host `SettingsPaneColumn` instead of a grouped `Form`; their scenarios and assertions are unchanged). Unrelated references that the record run also rewrote were restored.
- **Executed after the change:** targeted run `SettingsWindowTests`, `CompressionProfileSettingsTests`, `ArchivePreviewLimitTests`, `SettingsSliderTests`, `SidebarSplitConstraintTests`: 53 tests, 53 passed, 0 failed. Full Unit plan (`Scripts/affected-tests.py --run --no-ui`): 435 Swift Testing cases passed, the one failure is the baseline `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests` timing; the crash/restart above lost some XCTest cases, as at the baseline. `Scripts/affected-tests.py --check`: 0 problems (`SettingsLayoutComponents.swift` mapped with the Settings rule).
- **UI tests: not run.** The offscreen tool ran five Settings-related UI tests (`SettingsUITests` ×3, `IconPreviewUITests` settings toggle, `LocalizationUITests`); every one stopped at `app.activate()` (“Failed to activate application … Running Background”, also in tests this change cannot affect), and an earlier attempt ended with a crash of the simulated app; no real-mode run was made. The list-column drag, profile open/save/delete and slider-drag UI tests therefore have **no evidence for this change**; the changed code keeps their identifiers (`settings-list-column-<column>` on the row with the handle at its leading edge, `settings-compression-profile-<name>`, `settings-*` toggles and pop-ups).
- **Not covered / pending:** a real-window look (the empty toolbar item that gives the 52 pt toolbar was verified only in a hosted offscreen window, not in the Settings scene; the glass sidebar and its selection are drawn by the window server and render wrongly offscreen, so the sidebar is checked as rows and by table metrics, and the offscreen sidebar is 144 pt wide by D-75 until its constraint applies); profile page still a grouped `Form`; VoiceOver of the switch rows; keyboard and focus order in the new rows; reduced motion and increased contrast.

## Settings in two columns — ZF-58, 2026-11-02 (D-74, D-75)

Contract: CURRENT-STATE “Settings window in two columns (ZF-58)” and Archive creation; UI-UX-GUIDE “Settings window in two columns — ZF-58”. Branch `feat/settings-two-columns` (base `c1c5d47`, commit `519a085`), merged into `main` after ZF-57 on 2026-11-02; ZF-57 and ZF-58 were both numbered ZF-57 in parallel worktrees, and this one was renumbered at the merge.

- **Baseline before the change** (`/tmp/zf-settings/baseline-unit.log`): `Unit` plan 652 tests, 1 failed — `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests.mixedDirectChildren` (250 ms bound, preexisting).
- **New suites:** `SettingsWindowTests` (8 tests, 14 cases: pane groups and symbols; pane strings in every language with exact Portuguese and German checks; remembered pane with fallback; pane contents; navigation model — choosing a pane closes a profile page, Duplicate replaces the page, the window title follows the pane; unified toolbar; detail column keeps the 568 pt form width for the size sliders; snapshots) and `CompressionProfileSettingsTests` (7 tests, 12 cases: summaries in English, German, Portuguese; Add Profile; Duplicate Profile with session password; session password pairs; name saved while typing through the real field editor and an emptied name restored; list and page snapshots). Three separate runs of both suites: 15/15 each.
- **Bug-reproducing tests (D-75):** `SidebarSplitConstraintTests` “Limits reset after they were applied are applied again…” failed without the correction (minimum 140, width 144) and passes with it; the empty-name case failed reproducibly before its correction and passes after it.
- **Snapshots (18 references, all reviewed):** View Options (light, dark German), Compression (light, dark, light Portuguese), Previews in Archives (light, dark), profile list (light, dark), profile page (ZIP light; 7z with password light and dark); Settings ▸ Previews in Archives section re-recorded ×4 (repeated title removed, D-74). General, Sidebar and Automation are not snapshotted: they show this Mac's Finder extension state, computer and home names and grants. The sidebar's glass material does not render offscreen (black selection, missing labels in the first attempt), so the pane snapshots show the detail column only.
- **Final `Unit` plan** (`/tmp/zf-settings/unit-final.log`): 667 tests, 3 failed — `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests` (timing bound), `TagCatalogTests` “File events update tags…” (`:1688`, FSEvents wait, D-49) and `ZforgeTests` “Rapid column archive selection…” (`:1096`, D-71 contention); load average about 17. Both of the latter pass alone; the tag test also fails with the two new suites skipped, and the column test passes twice run together with them, so neither is caused by this change.
- **Offscreen UI** (`affected-20261102-152132`, 3 lanes, no Automation Mode): **16/16 passed**, including `SettingsUITests.testCompressionProfileOpensSavesAndDeletes` (new: column widths 220/568 and one-line English sidebar titles; Add Profile, renaming, toolbar back button, row push, Delete confirmation Cancel and Delete, last profile not deletable) and the four tests updated to choose their pane (`testListColumnsReorderByDragging`, `testArchiveSizeLimitSliderHoldsRoundValuesWhileDragging`, `LocalizationUITests`, `IconPreviewUITests`). Screen guard: 0 visible windows, 0 synthetic events.
- **Real UI (announced; took over the screen):** final run 6/6 passed — both `AutomationUITests`, `testCustomCompressionPanelOpensFolderChooser`, `testLocalDragsOptionCopyAndConfirmAdditionToArchive`, `testArchiveDragCopiesIntoSidebarFolder`, `testArchiveInternalCopyConfirmationKeepsHistoryAndAncestorColumns` (the sidebar bridge is shared with the browser). Two earlier real runs (7 and 3 tests, all passed) produced the screenshots that revealed D-75.
- **Visual review (real screenshots):** Compression pane and profile page (English, dark), General in Portuguese and Bengali, View Options, Automation — after D-75: 220 pt sidebar, one-line titles in English and Portuguese, back button beside the title.
- **Not covered / pending:** VoiceOver reading of the sidebar rows and hints and the profile rows; keyboard navigation of the sidebar; reduced motion and increased contrast; light appearance and German in a real window; native review of the 12 new translations (TASKS ZF-58).
- **Profile rows without icons (user request, 2026-11-02, after the first review in the user's build):** the archive/lock tile was removed from the Compression profile rows. Re-recorded and reviewed: `CompressionProfileSettingsTests` list snapshots (light, dark) and `SettingsWindowTests` Compression pane snapshots (light, dark, pt-BR); the other pane and profile-page references are byte-identical. Both suites then passed against the new references (15 tests). The full `Unit` plan and UI tests were not rerun for this visual-only change.
- **Refined tiles and no pane header (user request, 2026-11-02):** new `SettingsWindowTests` “Each pane's tile has its own colour…” (fixed sRGB gradient, lighter at the top, same in light and dark, distinct per pane) and “A pane shows no header…” (the pane form holds only its content). Re-recorded and reviewed: the 7 `SettingsWindowTests` pane snapshots and the 2 profile-list snapshots (no header); the profile pages and `ArchivePreviewLimitTests` references are byte-identical. The sidebar tiles are not in an offscreen snapshot (glass sidebar); they were reviewed in a SwiftUI render at 20 pt in light and dark (`/tmp/zf-icons/sidebar.png`) and are pending the user's look in their build. **Full `Unit` plan:** 669 tests, 3 failed, all load-sensitive and recorded before: `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests` (timing bound), `TagCatalogTests` “File events update tags…” (FSEvents wait, D-49) and `IconPreviewGridTests` “Icons show content…” (`folder` snapshot: `Preview.html`'s thumbnail drawn without its text, the D-71 symptom; the suite then passed twice alone, 6/6). **Offscreen UI:** every selected test passed (`affected-20261102-183937*`, including `SettingsUITests` ×2, `LocalizationUITests`, `IconPreviewUITests`; screen guard 0 front changes, 0 synthetic events). The real-only group was not rerun: the change touches only the Settings tiles, header and sidebar caption, none of which those tests (Finder, drags, open panels, Automation grants) reach beyond the unchanged `settings-pane-*` identifiers.
- **Symbols chosen by the user (2026-11-02)** from rendered alternatives (`/tmp/zf-icons/alternatives.png`, light and dark, 44 and 20 pt): General `gear`, View Options `eye.fill` (kept), Previews in Archives `rectangle.and.text.magnifyingglass`. `SettingsWindowTests` passed (10 tests, including that every pane symbol exists); no snapshot shows the sidebar tiles, so no reference changed. The look in the user's build is pending their review.
- **After merging ZF-57 and ZF-58 into `main` (`84f85df`, 2026-11-02):** the warning gate built both merge commits with 0 warnings (38 s and 80 s); `python3 -m unittest discover -s Scripts/tests` OK; `affected-tests.py --check` 28 UI tests, 22 rules, 0 problems. **Full `Unit` plan** (`/tmp/zf-settings/unit-main.log`): 669 tests, 3 failed, all load-sensitive and recorded before — `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests` (timing bound), `ZforgeTests` “Rapid column archive selection…” (D-71 contention) and `TagCatalogTests` “File events update tags…” (FSEvents wait, D-49). UI tests were not rerun after the merge: the branches' UI results above stand, and ZF-57 changes no app code.

## Keyboard Shortcuts window redesign — ZF-59, 2026-11-09 (D-76)

Contract: CURRENT-STATE “Keyboard Shortcuts window redesign (ZF-59)” and UI-UX-GUIDE “Shortcut Reference”. Branch `feat/redesign-shortcuts-window` (base `6be2991`). Presentation change only: catalog data, the search contract (`sections(matching:locale:)`) and the Help menu entry are unchanged.

- **New suites.** `ShortcutKeyCapsTests` (9 tests): glyph splitting (`⌘Z`, `⌃⌥⌘N`, `⌥⌘⌫`, `⌘[`, `⌘−`, `⇧⌘.`), word keys (`Return`, `Space`), alternatives (`⌘+|⌘=`, `Space|⌘Y`), pointer gestures (the five catalog strings), the whole catalog parses and only Mouse and Drag carries gestures, spoken form in English and German (`Shift Command N`, `Space or Command Y`, `Umschalt Befehl N`), category filtering (every section alone, All Shortcuts, catalog-total counts), search inside a category, category presentation (symbols exist, localized titles, tile colours). `KeyboardShortcutsSnapshotTests` (5 tests, 9 references).
- **Last targeted run** (`ShortcutKeyCapsTests`, `KeyboardShortcutsSnapshotTests`, `KeyboardShortcutHelpTests`, `SettingsWindowTests`, `FileMenuLocalizationTests`): 31 tests in 5 suites, 29 passed; the only issues are the two dark `SettingsWindowTests` snapshots that fail identically on clean `main` (D-76). The three existing `KeyboardShortcutHelpTests` search tests pass untouched; its Portuguese snapshot reference was re-recorded for the approved redesign (it hosts the real `NavigationSplitView`, whose sidebar does not draw offscreen, so that image shows the detail column only).
- **Snapshots (10 references, all viewed before committing):** `ZforgeSnapshots/ShortcutKeyCapsTests/` `allShortcuts.light|dark`, `singleCategory.light|dark` (View), `mouseCategory.light|dark`, `germanNarrow.light` (All Shortcuts, 600 pt wide, German: “Dateien und Objekte” wraps to two lines, nothing clipped) and `germanNarrow.mouse-dark` (long German actions truncate with an ellipsis, gestures intact), `noResults.light`; plus `ZforgeSnapshots/KeyboardShortcutHelpTests/portugueseSnapshot.portuguese-folder-selection.png`. Spacing was checked against the mockup values (52 pt title band; first header 22 pt below it; 20 pt margins; 32 pt rows; 20 pt caps with 3 pt gap; 206 pt sidebar). **Limit:** `NavigationSplitView` and the sidebar `List` do not draw offscreen, so the snapshots compose the real category rows (with a drawn selection) and the real detail view in a plain two-column layout under a stand-in title band; the real sidebar material and selection, the sidebar search field, the toolbar title and the Divider row between All Shortcuts and the sections were not seen.
- **Warning gate:** `Scripts/check-warnings.py` on the staged tree reports 0 warnings (see the commit).
- **Not executed:** every UI test. `Scripts/affected-tests.py --run` stops after the failing `Unit` plan; running its offscreen part directly, all 14 selected offscreen tests failed at `launchWithBrowserWindow` (“Failed to activate application … Running Background”), and the same single test fails the same way on clean `main` (D-76). The two selected real-only UI tests were not run (they take over the screen). `testKeyboardShortcutHelpOpensAndFilters` was changed to find the window by its new title, “All Shortcuts” (the window is titled by the selected category), and is therefore **unverified**: whether XCTest exposes the SwiftUI window by that title, and finds the sidebar search field with `searchFields.firstMatch`, has to be confirmed in a run.
- **Full `Unit` plan (superseded by ZF-64, D-81, which corrected the crash):** no trustworthy totals; the host crashes mid-plan on this machine, with and without ZF-59 (D-76). Complete runs on `main` and with ZF-59 are listed there.
- **Not covered / pending:** the real window (sidebar material and selection, search placement, per-category window title, light/dark), VoiceOver reading of rows and key caps (only the spoken strings are tested), reduced motion, increased contrast, text-size scaling of the caps, native-speaker review of the 12 new translations. The user's look at the result is pending.

## Unit test host crash — ZF-64, 2026-11-09 (D-81, D-82)

Environment: macOS 27.2 (26B123), Xcode 27.2 (27B52); runs alone on the Mac. Logs and result bundles are in the session scratchpad (`…/scratchpad/{zombie1,zombie2,unit1,unit2,snapb}`).
- **Before the fix (`6be2991`):** 18 crash reports of 2026-11-09 with the same `objc_release` ← autorelease pool pop ← main-actor job stack. Full `Unit` plan with `NSZombieEnabled=YES`, `MallocScribble=1`, `MallocStackLogging=lite` in a copy of the `.xctestrun` (`zombie1`): `-[NSKVONotifying_NSWindow release]: message sent to deallocated instance` right after the “Preview selection order” suite, host restarted, 669 tests / 474 passed / 195 failed. `PreviewSelectionOrderTests` + `HTMLPreviewTests` alone with zombies (5 tests): the same zombie in 1 of 2 runs and in 1 of 8 runs; the single test or `HTMLPreviewTests` alone: 0 of 4 runs each (the over-release needs another holder of the window to release last).
- **Reproduction test:** `TestWindowLifetimeTests` (2 tests, deterministic and crash-free): passed 3 of 3 runs alone. It shows that a window left at AppKit's default loses one reference on `close()`, and that a `TestWindow.make` window loses none, survives two closes and is freed once when its last reference goes.
- **After the fix, zombies on (`zombie2`):** full plan, no zombie message, no host restart; 671 tests / 665 passed / 6 failed (below). Subset of 5 tests with zombies: 0 crashes in 8 runs, 5/5 passed each time.
- **After the fix, plain `xcodebuild test -testPlan Unit` (`unit1`, `unit2`):** no crash report, no host restart. `unit1`: 671 tests / 665 passed / 6 failed; `unit2`: 671 / 667 / 4. Failures: the three D-82 snapshot tests (both runs); `ArchiveDiscoveryPerformanceTests` timing bound (both runs; load-sensitive, recorded before); `TagCatalogTests` “File events update tags…” (D-49) and `ZforgeTests` “Rapid column archive selection…” (D-71 contention) in `unit1` only, both load-sensitive and recorded before.
- **D-82 baseline:** the three snapshot suites (32 tests) fail with the same five dark cases on unmodified `main` rebuilt in this worktree (`snapb`) and twice with the fix (`snap1`, `snap2`), so the fix did not cause them.
- **UI tests:** none selected (`Scripts/affected-tests.py`: 5 changed files, UI tests 0); `--check` 28 UI tests, 22 rules, 0 problems. No real UI test was run. The offscreen `app.activate()` “Running Background” failure was not investigated; it does not share this cause.
- **Not covered / pending:** why macOS 27.2 turned the latent over-release into a near-certain crash (the 27.0.1 system is not available to compare); the `AutomationPermissionUIFixture` window and the D-82 references (TASKS ZF-65).
